### [1] [Contrastive Multi-Modal Hypergraph Reasoning for 3D Crowd Mesh Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13854)
*Minghao Sun,Chongyang Xu,Yitao Xie,Buzhen Huang,Kun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出对比多模态超图推理方法(CoMHR)，通过融合RGB特征、几何先验和遮挡感知姿态，构建共享拓扑超图建模高阶人群动态，并采用超图对比学习增强模态内判别性和模态间正交性，在Panoptic和GigaCrowd数据集上实现多人物3D重建的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单模态方法缺乏几何引导，且孤立重建个体忽视了群体上下文信息，难以应对严重遮挡和深度模糊问题。

Method: 1) 多模态节点初始化：融合RGB特征、几何先验和遮挡感知的不完整姿态；2) 盆骨深度指示器作为全局空间锚点，对齐视觉特征与深度排序；3) 构建共享拓扑超图，超越成对约束建模高阶人群动态；4) 超图对比学习，联合增强模态内判别性和跨模态正交性。

Result: 在Panoptic和GigaCrowd基准上达到新的最优性能。

Conclusion: 多模态融合与超图推理能有效传播全局上下文，即使在严重遮挡下也能推断缺失信息，为人群3D重建提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Multi-person 3D reconstruction is pivotal for real-world interaction analysis, yet remains challenging due to severe occlusions and depth ambiguity. Current approaches typically rely on single-modality inputs, which inherently lack geometric guidance. Furthermore, these methods often reconstruct subjects in isolation, neglecting the collective group context essential for resolving ambiguities in crowded scenes. To address these limitations, we propose Contrastive Multi-modal Hypergraph Reasoning to synergize semantic, geometric, and pose cues for crowd reconstruction. We first initialize robust node representations by combining RGB features, geometric priors, and occlusion-aware incomplete poses. Additionally, we introduce a pelvis depth indicator as a global spatial anchor, aligning visual features with a metric-scale-agnostic depth ordering. Subsequently, we construct a shared-topology hypergraph that moves beyond pairwise constraints to model higher-order crowd dynamics. To improve feature fusion, we design a hypergraph-based contrastive learning scheme that jointly enhances intra-modal discriminability and enforces cross-modal orthogonality. This mechanism enables the network to propagate global context effectively, allowing it to infer missing information even under severe occlusion. Extensive experiments on the Panoptic and GigaCrowd benchmarks confirm that our method achieves new state-of-the-art performance. Code and pre-trained models are available at https://github.com/SunMH-try/CoMHR.

</details>


### [2] [Few Channels Draw The Whole Picture: Revealing Massive Activations in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13974)
*Evelyn Turri,Davide Bucciarelli,Sara Sarto,Lorenzo Baraldi,Marcella Cornia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Diffusion Transformers (DiTs)中"大规模激活"通道的三个核心特性：功能关键性（破坏这些通道会导致生成质量崩溃）、空间组织性（聚类这些通道可形成与主体和显著区域对齐的语义分区）以及可迁移性（在不同提示间传输这些激活可实现语义插值）。基于这些特性，本文提出了无额外训练的提示插值和主体驱动生成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管DiT和基于流的架构已成为最强的文生图模型，但提示如何塑造图像语义的内部机制仍不清楚。本文旨在揭示这一内部机制。

Method: 通过分析DiT中隐藏状态通道的响应统计特性，识别出"大规模激活"通道子集，并采用三类探针实验：1) 破坏探针（归零化大规模通道验证功能关键性）；2) 空间聚类分析（基于图像流令牌的大规模激活进行聚类）；3) 跨提示激活传输（将源提示的大规模激活迁移到目标提示轨迹中）。

Result: 1) 归零大规模通道导致生成质量骤降，而破坏同等规模的低统计量通道影响甚微；2) 聚类大规模激活可获得与图像主体和显著区域高度对齐的语义分区；3) 跨提示传输大规模激活可在保持目标内容的同时向源提示语义偏移，实现局部语义插值。

Conclusion: 大规模激活并非激活异常值，而是一种稀疏的、受提示条件控制的载体子空间，负责组织和控制DiT模型中的语义信息。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) and related flow-based architectures are now among the strongest text-to-image generators, yet the internal mechanisms through which prompts shape image semantics remain poorly understood. In this work, we study massive activations: a small subset of hidden-state channels whose responses are consistently much larger than the rest. We show that, despite their sparsity, these few channels effectively draw the whole picture, in three complementary senses. First, they are functionally critical: a controlled disruption probe that zeroes the massive channels causes a sharp collapse in generation quality, while disrupting an equally-sized set of low-statistic channels has marginal effect. Second, they are spatially organized: restricting image-stream tokens to massive channels and clustering them yields coherent partitions that closely align with the main subject and salient regions, exposing a structured spatial code hidden inside an apparently outlier-like subspace. Third, they are transferable: transporting massive activations from one prompt-conditioned trajectory into another, shifts the final image toward the source prompt while preserving substantial content from the target, producing localized semantic interpolation rather than unstructured pixel blending. We exploit this property in two use cases: text-conditioned and image-conditioned semantic transport, where massive activations transport enables prompt interpolation and subject-driven generation without any additional training. Together, these results recast massive activations not as activation anomalies, but as a sparse prompt-conditioned carrier subspace that organizes and controls semantic information in modern DiT models.

</details>


### [3] [CineMesh4D: Personalized 4D Whole Heart Reconstruction from Sparse Cine MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13994)
*Xiaoyue Liu,Xiaohan Yuan,Mark Y Chan,Ching-Hui Sia,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CineMesh4D，一种端到端的4D（3D+t）流水线，通过可微渲染损失和双上下文时序模块，从多视图2D电影MRI直接重建患者特异性全心网格，在重建质量和运动一致性上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心脏图像到网格方法通常只重建部分心腔或单个心动周期，无法同时解决2D切片对3D心脏解剖的稀疏采样以及心脏形态与运动紧密耦合的问题。

Method: 1）提出可微渲染损失，实现从多视图稀疏轮廓监督3D+t全心网格；2）设计双上下文时序模块，融合全局和局部心脏时序信息以捕获高维序列模式。

Result: 在定量和定性评估中，CineMesh4D在重建质量和运动一致性方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: CineMesh4D为个性化实时心脏评估提供了实用途径，代码将在论文接收后开源。

Abstract: Accurate 3D+t whole-heart mesh reconstruction from cine MRI is a clinically crucial yet technically challenging task. The difficulty of this task arises from two coupled factors: inherently sparse sampling of 3D cardiac anatomy by 2D image slices and the tight coupling between cardiac shape and motion. Current cardiac image-to-mesh approaches typically reconstruct only a subset of cardiac chambers or a single phase of the cardiac cycle. In this work, we propose CineMesh4D, a novel end-to-end 4D (3D+t) pipeline that directly reconstructs patient-specific whole-heart mesh from multi-view 2D cine MRI via cross-domain mapping. Specifically, we introduce a differentiable rendering loss that enables supervision of 3D+t whole-heart mesh from multi-view sparse contours of cine MRI. Furthermore, we develop a dual-context temporal block that fuses global and local cardiac temporal information to capture high-dimensional sequential patterns. In quantitative and qualitative evaluations, CineMesh4D outperforms existing approaches in terms of reconstruction quality and motion consistency, providing a practical pathway for personalized real-time cardiac assessment. The code will be publicly released once the manuscript is accepted.

</details>


### [4] [Unified Pix Token And Word Token Generative Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14028)
*Haun Leung,ZiNan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一图像像素(token)和文本词(token)的新生成式语言模型，通过像素级token嵌入、颜色折叠、全局条件注意力近似和无监督图像预训练等方法，解决了现有ViT（如CLIP/SigLIP）在视觉细节理解（如小文本、数字识别）上的局限性。实验表明该模型在小模型和有限数据下表现良好，并符合缩放定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流开源多模态模型使用CLIP或SigLIP方法获得的ViT作为视觉编码器，导致对图像细节（如小文本、数字）的视觉理解能力受限，亟需一种能更好地统一视觉与语言表示的生成式模型。

Method: 1) 将图像像素(token)与文本词(token)统一到生成式语言模型中；2) 每个图像像素拥有独立的token嵌入；3) 颜色折叠（color folding）技术；4) 全局条件注意力近似（global conditional attention approximation）；5) 图像无监督预训练。

Result: 在小模型参数和有限训练数据条件下，该模型展现出良好的性能表现，且验证了其符合缩放定律（scaling law），随着模型参数和训练数据增加性能持续提升。

Conclusion: 提出的像素-词统一生成模型在视觉细节理解上优于现有ViT方案，具备良好的扩展性，未来通过增大模型和数据规模有望持续提升性能。

Abstract: Since the emergence of Vision Transformer (ViT), it has been widely used in generative language model and generative visual model. Especially in the current state-of-art open source multimodal models, ViT obtained by CLIP or SigLIP method serves as the vision encoder backbone to help them acquire visual understanding capabilities. But this method leads to limitations in visual understanding for details, such as difficulty in recognizing small text or numbers in images. To address these issues, we propose a new model to unify pix token and word token into the generative language model. The new model also features with each pix of image having its own token embedding, color folding, global conditional attention approximation and image unsupervised pretraining. We conducted image unsupervised pretraining experiments using our new model to explore its potential. The experimental results show that it has good performance even in small model and with limited training data. We believe our model also conforms to the scaling law, as long as model parameters and training data increased, its performance will continue to improve.

</details>


### [5] [Evolving Layer-Specific Scalar Functions for Hardware-Aware Transformer Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14047)
*Kieran Carrigg,Sigur de Vries,Amirhossein Sadough,Marcel van Gerven*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于遗传编程的硬件感知框架，为视觉Transformer中的归一化层演化出异构的、层特定的标量函数近似，结合训练后重对齐策略，无需重新训练即可消除层归一化的全局归约瓶颈，在ImageNet-1K上仅用20个epoch恢复84.25%的Top-1精度，实现了计算复杂度与片外内存流量之间的有利权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformer在边缘设备部署时面临计算复杂度和层归一化带来的全局归约瓶颈问题。现有方法用硬件友好的标量近似替换归一化层，但同质化替换无法最优适配所有层的行为，且依赖昂贵的模型重训。

Method: 提出一个硬件感知框架，利用遗传编程从预训练权重中直接演化出异构的、层特定的标量函数来近似归一化行为；同时设计了一种新颖的训练后重对齐策略，完全无需从头重训模型。

Result: 演化出的表达式精确近似目标归一化行为，捕获91.6%的方差（R²），远超同质化基线的70.2%；仅用20个epoch即可恢复84.25%的ImageNet-1K Top-1精度。

Conclusion: 该方法在消除全局归约瓶颈的同时保持了模型性能，建立了算术复杂度与片外内存流量之间的高度有利权衡，为ViT在边缘加速器上的高效部署扫除了主要障碍。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) achieve state-of-the-art performance on challenging vision tasks, but their deployment on edge devices is severely hindered by the computational complexity and global reduction bottleneck imposed by layer normalization. Recent methods attempt to bypass this by replacing normalization layers with hardware-friendly scalar approximations. However, these homogeneous replacements do not optimally fit to all layers' behaviour and rely on expensive model retraining. In this work, we propose a highly efficient, hardware-aware framework that utilizes genetic programming (GP) to evolve heterogeneous, layer-specific scalar functions directly from pre-trained weights. Coupled with a novel post-training re-alignment strategy, our approach eliminates the need to retrain models from scratch entirely. Our evolved expressions accurately approximate the target normalization behaviours, capturing $91.6\%$ of the variance ($R^2$) compared to only $70.2\%$ for homogeneous baselines, allowing our modified architecture to recover $84.25\%$ Top-1 ImageNet-1K accuracy in only 20 epochs. By preserving this performance while eliminating the global reduction bottleneck, our approach establishes a highly favourable trade-off between arithmetic complexity and off-chip memory traffic, removing a primary barrier to the efficient deployment of ViTs on edge accelerators.

</details>


### [6] [CurveBench: A Benchmark for Exact Topological Reasoning over Nested Jordan Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14068)
*Amirreza Mohseni,Mona Mohammadi,Morteza Saghafian,Naser Talebizadeh Saradari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CurveBench基准，用于评估视觉层次拓扑推理能力。包含756张非相交Jordan曲线图像，任务是从图像中恢复完整的包含关系树。最强模型Gemini 3.1 Pro在Easy和Hard上分别仅达71.1%和19.1%的树生成准确率。通过RLVR微调，Qwen3-VL-8B在Easy上从2.8%提升至33.3%，超越GPT-5.4和Claude Opus 4.5，但Hard任务仍远未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉推理基准缺乏对层次拓扑结构的精确理解能力评估，而拓扑包含关系是视觉场景理解的基础但尚未被充分研究。

Method: 构建包含756张图像的CurveBench基准，涵盖Easy、Polygonal、Topographic、Maze、Dense五种难度配置。任务定义为结构化预测：从图像中预测根化的包含关系树。使用RLVR风格微调对开源视觉语言模型进行训练。

Result: Gemini 3.1 Pro在Easy上71.1%，Hard上19.1%。Qwen3-VL-8B经RLVR微调后Easy准确率从2.8%提升至33.3%，超越GPT-5.4和Claude Opus 4.5。

Conclusion: 精确的拓扑感知视觉推理仍然远未解决，尤其在Hard场景下存在巨大提升空间。

Abstract: We introduce CurveBench, a benchmark for hierarchical topological reasoning from visual input. CurveBench consists of \textbf{756 images} of pairwise non-intersecting Jordan curves across easy, polygonal, topographic-inspired, maze-like, and dense counting configurations. Each image is annotated with a rooted tree encoding the containment relations between planar regions. We formulate the task as structured prediction: given an image, a model must recover the full rooted containment tree induced by the curves. Despite the visual simplicity of the task, the strongest evaluated model, Gemini 3.1 Pro, achieves only \textbf{71.1\%} tree-generation accuracy on CurveBench-Easy and \textbf{19.1\%} on CurveBench-Hard. We further demonstrate benchmark utility through RLVR-style fine-tuning of open-weight vision-language models. Our trained Qwen3-VL-8B model improves over \texttt{Qwen-3-VL-8B-Thinking} from \textbf{2.8\%} to \textbf{33.3\%} tree-generation accuracy on CurveBench-Easy, exceeding GPT-5.4 and Claude Opus 4.5 under our evaluation protocol. The remaining gap, especially on CurveBench-Hard, shows that exact topology-aware visual reasoning remains far from solved.

</details>


### [7] [Venus-DeFakerOne: Unified Fake Image Detection & Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14091)
*GuangJian Team*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DeFakerOne，一个基于数据驱动的统一伪造图像检测与定位基础模型，融合InternVL2和SAM2，在39个检测和9个定位基准上达到SOTA性能，并系统分析了跨域伪影迁移、数据缩放规律等设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI的快速发展使得图像伪造方式趋于统一，但现有伪造图像检测与定位（FIDL）研究仍处于碎片化状态，导致统一的伪造生成机制与领域特定的检测范式之间出现不匹配。

Method: 提出DeFakerOne，一种数据驱动的统一FIDL基础模型，融合InternVL2（视觉-语言模型）和SAM2（分割模型），同时支持图像级检测和像素级伪造定位。

Result: 在39个伪造检测基准和9个定位基准上达到SOTA性能；对现实扰动和最新生成器（如GPT-Image-2）表现出优异的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 系统分析了数据缩放规律、跨域伪影迁移-干扰模式、细粒度监督的必要性以及原始分辨率伪影保留等设计原则，为构建可扩展、鲁棒且统一的FIDL模型提供了指导。

Abstract: In recent years, the rapid evolution of generative AI has fundamentally reshaped the paradigm of image forgery, breaking the traditional boundaries between document editing, natural image manipulation, DeepFake generation, and full-image AIGC synthesis. Despite this shift toward unified forgery generation, existing research in Fake Image Detection and Localization (FIDL) remains fragmented. This creates a mismatch between increasingly unified forgery generation mechanisms and the domain-specific detection paradigm. Bridging this mismatch poses two key challenges for FIDL: understanding cross-domain artifacts transfer and interference, and building a high-capacity unified foundation model for joint detection and localization. To address these challenges, we propose DeFakerOne, a data-centric, unified FIDL foundation model integrating InternVL2 and SAM2. DeFakerOne enables simultaneous image-level detection and pixel-level forgery localization across diverse scenarios. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DeFakerOne achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming baselines on 39 forgery detection benchmarks and 9 localization benchmarks. Furthermore, the model exhibits superior robustness against real-world perturbations and state-of-the-art generators such as GPT-Image-2. Finally, we provide a systematic analysis of data scaling laws, cross-domain artifacts transfer-interference patterns, the necessity of fine-grained supervision, and the original resolution artifacts preservation, highlighting the design principles for scalable, robust, and unified FIDL.

</details>


### [8] [DUET: Dual-Paradigm Adaptive Expert Triage with Single-cell Inductive Prior for Spatial Transcriptomics Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14104)
*Junchao Zhu,Ruining Deng,Junlin Guo,Tianyuan Yao,Chongyu Qu,Juming Xiong,Zhengyi Lu,Yanfan Zhu,Marilyn Lionts,Yuechen Yang,Yu Wang,Shilin Zhao,Haichun Yang,Yuankai Huo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DUET提出了一种双范式框架，结合参数化预测和基于记忆的检索，并利用大规模单细胞参考数据作为生物约束，从组织学图像中推断空间分辨基因表达，在三个公共数据集上达到了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将组织学图像到基因表达的推断简化为简单的形态-表达映射，视觉相似性无法保证分子一致性；单细胞数据资源丰富但未被充分利用于视觉-组学建模；当前方法采用单一范式，存在瓶颈，无法平衡表达灵活性与生物保真度。

Method: DUET采用双范式（dual-paradigm）框架：并行实现参数化回归（parametric prediction）和基于记忆的检索（memory-based retrieval），并自适应协调两条互补路径的输出。引入大规模单细胞参考数据作为分子状态的生物学约束，以减轻视觉歧义。设计轻量级适配器（lightweight adapter）动态分配不同空间上下文下的分支偏好。

Result: 在三个公共数据集上，DUET在所有基因尺度上均取得了SOTA性能，每个提出的组件都贡献了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: DUET通过双范式协同、单细胞数据约束和动态适配机制，有效解决了现有方法在视觉-基因映射中的瓶颈问题，为空间转录组学的计算补全提供了新范式。

Abstract: Inferring spatially resolved gene expression from histology images offers a cost-effective complement to spatial transcriptomics (ST). However, existing methods reduce this task to a simple morphology-to-expression mapping, where visual similarity does not guarantee molecular consistency. Meanwhile, single-cell data has amassed rich resources far surpassing the scale of ST data, yet it remains underexplored in vision-omics modeling. Furthermore, current approaches commit to a monolithic paradigm with bottlenecks, unable to balance expressive flexibility with biological fidelity. To bridge these gaps, we propose DUET, a novel dual-paradigm framework that synergizes parametric prediction and memory-based retrieval under cellular inductive priors. DUET implements a parallel regression-retrieval paradigm, adaptively reconciling the outputs of its complementary pathways. To mitigate aleatoric vision ambiguity, we incorporate large-scale single-cell references to impose molecular states as biological constraints for faithful learning. Building upon structural refinement, we further design a lightweight adapter to dynamically assign branch preference across spatial contexts to achieve optimal performance. Extensive experiments on three public datasets across varied gene scales demonstrate that DUET achieves SOTA performance, with consistent gains contributed by each proposed component. Code is available at https://github.com/Junchao-Zhu/DUET

</details>


### [9] [Delta Forcing: Trust Region Steering for Interactive Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14382)
*Yuheng Wu,Xiangbo Gao,Tianhao Chen,Xinghao Chen,Qing Yin,Zhengzhong Tu,Dongman Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Delta Forcing框架，通过自适应信任区域约束不可靠的教师监督，平衡视频生成的响应性与时间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归视频生成方法在响应新事件时容易产生漂移，原因是教师模型提供条件对齐但轨迹无关的指导，导致局部有效但全局不一致的生成偏差。

Method: 借鉴信任区域策略优化（TRPO）思想，通过估计教师与生成器轨迹间的潜在空间delta来衡量过渡一致性，并以此平衡教师监督与单调连续性目标，在自适应信任区域内约束不可靠的教师指导。

Result: 实验表明Delta Forcing在保持事件响应性的同时显著提升了时间一致性。

Conclusion: Delta Forcing通过自适应约束教师监督，有效解决了自回归视频生成中响应性与稳定性的权衡问题。

Abstract: Interactive real-time autoregressive video generation is essential for applications such as content creation and world modeling, where visual content must adapt to dynamically evolving event conditions. A fundamental challenge lies in balancing reactivity and stability: models must respond promptly to new events while maintaining temporal coherence over long horizons. Existing approaches distill bidirectional models into autoregressive generators and further adapt them via streaming long tuning, yet often exhibit persistent drift after condition changes. We identify the cause as conditional bias, where the teacher may provide condition-aligned but trajectory-agnostic guidance, biasing generation toward locally valid yet globally inconsistent modes. Inspired by Trust Region Policy Optimization, we propose Delta Forcing, a simple yet effective framework that constrains unreliable teacher supervision within an adaptive trust region. Specifically, Delta Forcing estimates transition consistency from the latent delta between teacher and generator trajectories, and uses it to balance teacher supervision with a monotonic continuity objective. This suppress unreliable teacher-induced shifts while preserving responsiveness to new events. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Delta Forcing significantly improves consistency while maintaining event reactivity.

</details>


### [10] [SceneForge: Structured World Supervision from 3D Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14399)
*Jizhizi Li,Jiayang Ao,Danny Wicks,Petru-Daniel Tudosiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneForge是一个基于可编辑3D世界状态的干预驱动框架，通过显式干预和场景依赖传播生成结构化的、跨编辑/视角/场景级干预一致的监督信号，实验表明该监督能有效提升多种视觉任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观测级数据集无法暴露底层场景状态及其变化传播方式，难以获取跨编辑、视角和场景级干预保持一致的监督信号。

Method: 将每个场景表示为一个持久化世界，包含语义、几何和物理依赖关系；通过显式干预（如物体移除、视角变化）并沿场景依赖传播其效果，渲染出与物体结构和场景级效应一致的监督信号（反事实观测、多视角观测、阴影和反射等效应感知信号）。使用Infinigen和Blender实例化，构建了包含大量反事实对和对齐标注的室内监督资源。

Result: 在匹配的训练预算下，SceneForge监督在物体移除和场景移除任务上均提升了多个基准的定量和定性性能。

Conclusion: 将监督建模为可编辑世界中的结构化状态变迁，为干预一致的多模态学习提供了实用且可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Many multimodal learning tasks require supervision that remains consistent across edits, viewpoints, and scene-level interventions. However, such supervision is difficult to obtain from observation-level datasets, which do not expose the underlying scene state or how changes propagate through it. We present SceneForge, an intervention-driven framework that generates structured supervision from editable 3D world states. SceneForge represents each scene as a persistent world with semantic, geometric, and physical dependencies. By applying explicit interventions (e.g., object removal or camera variation) and propagating their effects through scene dependencies, SceneForge renders supervision that remains consistent with object structure and scene-level effects. This produces aligned outputs including counterfactual observations, multi-view observations, and effect-aware signals such as shadows and reflections, all derived from a shared world state rather than post hoc image-space processing. We instantiate SceneForge using Infinigen and Blender to construct a licensing-clean indoor supervision resource with a large number of counterfactual pairs and aligned annotations from over 2K scenes, covering both diverse single-view and registered multi-view settings. Under matched training budgets, incorporating SceneForge supervision improves both object removal and scene removal performance across multiple benchmarks in both quantitative and qualitative evaluation. These results indicate that modeling supervision as structured state transitions in editable worlds provides a practical and scalable foundation for intervention-consistent multimodal learning.

</details>


### [11] [SToRe3D: Sparse Token Relevance in ViTs for Efficient Multi-View 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14110)
*Sandro Papais,Lezhou Feng,Charles Cossette,Lingting Ge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SToRe3D是一个面向Vision Transformer的2D图像标记与3D对象查询联合稀疏化框架，通过互相关注意力头筛选关键内容并存储过滤特征以供重新激活，在nuScenes基准上实现高达3倍推理加速且精度损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformer在多视图3D检测中性能强劲，但由于密集的标记和查询处理导致推理延迟过高；现有稀疏化方法主要针对2D视觉，无法扩展至全模型稀疏或处理3D对象查询。

Method: 提出SToRe3D框架，设计互相关注意力头联合选择2D图像标记和3D对象查询，将计算资源分配给驾驶关键内容，并存储过滤后的特征以便后续重新激活。

Result: 在nuScenes和新提出的nuScenes-Relevance基准上，实现高达3倍推理加速，精度损失极小，在规划关键智能体上保持准确性。

Conclusion: SToRe3D实现了实时大规模ViT-based 3D检测，验证了联合稀疏化2D和3D token的有效性。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) enable strong multi-view 3D detection but are limited by high inference latency from dense token and query processing across multiple views and large 3D regions. Existing sparsity methods, designed mainly for 2D vision, prune or merge image tokens but do not extend to full-model sparsity or address 3D object queries. We introduce SToRe3D, a relevance-aligned sparsity framework that jointly selects 2D image tokens and 3D object queries while storing filtered features for reactivation. Mutual 2D-3D relevance heads allocate compute to driving-critical content and preserve other embeddings. Evaluated on nuScenes and our new nuScenes-Relevance benchmark, SToRe3D achieves up to 3x faster inference with marginal accuracy loss, establishing real-time large-scale ViT-based 3D detection while maintaining accuracy on planning-critical agents.

</details>


### [12] [TOPOS: High-Fidelity and Efficient Industry-Grade 3D Head Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14594)
*Bojun Xiong,Zoubin Bi,Xinghui Peng,Yunmu Wang,Junchen Deng,Jun Liang,Jing Li,Bowen Cai,Huan Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TOPOS是一个面向工业级数字人制作的3D头部生成框架，能从单张图像生成具有固定参考拓扑的高保真头部网格和可重新照明的UV纹理，在顶点级一致性、几何和外观质量上均达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D头部生成方法生成的三角网格拓扑不一致、顶点数多，难以满足影视、动画、游戏工业管线中对固定参考拓扑（用于绑定、蒙皮、动画）的需求，阻碍了语义对应和资产复用。

Method: 提出TOPOS-VAE，受多模态大语言模型启发，利用Perceiver Resampler将不同拓扑的头部点云转换为统一参考拓扑；在此基础上训练TOPOS-DiT（整流流Transformer）从单图高效生成高质量头部网格；并设计TOPOS-Texture模块，通过微调多模态图像生成模型生成与网格几何对齐的可重新照明UV纹理图。

Result: 在3D头部生成任务上超越经典面部重建方法和通用3D对象生成模型，达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: TOPOS证明了在工业标准拓扑下进行单图像条件3D头部生成的可行性和优越性，为数字人创作提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: High-fidelity 3D head generation plays a crucial role in the film, animation and video game industries. In industrial pipelines, studios typically enforce a fixed reference topology across all head assets, as such a clean and uniform topology is a prerequisite for production-level rigging, skinning and animation. In this paper, we present TOPOS, a framework tailored for single image conditioned 3D head generation that jointly recovers geometry and appearance under such an industry-standard topology. In contrast to general 3D generative models which produce triangle meshes with inconsistent topology and numerous vertices, hindering semantic correspondence and asset-level reuse, TOPOS generates head meshes with a fixed, studio-style topology, enabling consistent vertex-level correspondence across all generated heads. To model heads under this unified topology, we proposed a novel variational autoencoder structure, termed TOPOS-VAE. Inspired by multi-model large language models (MLLMs), our TOPOS-VAE leverages the Perceiver Resampler to convert input pointclouds sampled from head meshes of diverse topologies into the target reference topology. Building upon TOPOS-VAE's structured latent space, we train a rectified flow transformer, TOPOS-DiT, to efficiently generate high-fidelity head meshes from a single image. We further present TOPOS-Texture, an end-to-end module that produces relightable UV texture maps from the same portrait image via fine-tuning a multimodal image generative model. The generated textures are spatially aligned with the underlying mesh geometry and faithfully preserve high-frequency appearance details. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TOPOS achieves state-of-the-art performance on 3D head generation, surpassing both classical face reconstruction methods and general 3D object generative models, highlighting its effectiveness for digital human creation.

</details>


### [13] [BioHuman: Learning Biomechanical Human Representations from Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14772)
*Yujun Huo,He Zhang,Chentao Song,Honglin Song,Zongyu Zuo,Tao Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于仿真的框架，从现有动作捕捉数据中估计肌肉激活，构建了包含同步视频、动作和肌肉激活的大规模数据集BioHuman10M，并在此基础上开发了端到端模型BioHuman，能够从单目视频输入联合预测人体运动与肌肉激活，实现了视觉观测与内部生物力学状态的有效桥接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体运动分析领域缺乏带有生物力学标注的大规模数据集，且现有方法无法从视觉观测直接推断内部生物力学状态（如肌肉激活），限制了运动分析、康复和损伤风险评估等应用的发展。

Method: 1) 提出基于仿真的框架，从现有动作捕捉数据估计肌肉激活，构建大规模数据集BioHuman10M；2) 提出端到端模型BioHuman，以单目视频为输入，联合预测人体运动与肌肉激活。

Result: BioHuman能够同时准确重建运动学层面的动作和肌肉活动，并在多种不同人体与动作场景下具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作为基于视频的生物力学理解建立了新的基准，为物理化的人体建模开辟了新的可能性。

Abstract: Understanding human motion beyond surface kinematics is crucial for motion analysis, rehabilitation, and injury risk assessment. However, progress in this domain is limited by the lack of large-scale datasets with biomechanical annotations, and by existing approaches that cannot directly infer internal biomechanical states from visual observations. In this paper, we introduce a simulation-based framework for estimating muscle activations from existing motion capture datasets, resulting in BioHuman10M, a large-scale dataset with synchronized video, motion, and activations. Building on BioHuman10M, we propose BioHuman, an end-to-end model that takes monocular video as input and jointly predicts human motion and muscle activations, effectively bridging visual observations and internal biomechanical states. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BioHuman enables accurate reconstruction of both kinematic motion and muscle activity, and generalizes across diverse subjects and motions. We believe our approach establishes a new benchmark for video-based biomechanical understanding and opens up new possibilities for physically grounded human modeling.

</details>


### [14] [PanoPlane: Plane-Aware Panoramic Completion for Sparse-View Indoor 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14135)
*Adil Qureshi,Dongki Jung,Jaehoon Choi,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PanoPlane提出了一种基于全景场景完成的稀疏视角室内新视角合成方法，通过训练无关的布局锚定注意力引导机制，利用360°全景扩散模型补全引导3D高斯泼溅重建，在仅需3张输入视图的情况下，在Replica、ScanNet++和Matterport3D数据集上实现了最高+17.8% PSNR的SOTA性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于透视的方法受限于有限视场角，生成训练视图时无法捕捉完整空间布局，导致不可观测区域的合成质量差。作者希望利用360°全景信息来约束生成过程，实现更准确的空间几何重建。

Method: 1) 采用360°全景扩散模型进行场景补全；2) 提出Layout Anchored Attention Steering（训练无关的注意力引导机制），在推理时将扩散模型内部注意力引导至检测到的平面表面，使不可观测区域的注意力指向几何一致的已观测内容；3) 用补全的全景图监督3D Gaussian Splatting训练。

Result: 在Replica、ScanNet++和Matterport3D数据集上，在3、6、9张输入视图设置下均达到SOTA，PSNR最高提升17.8%，且无需对扩散模型进行任何训练或微调。

Conclusion: 通过全景场景补全和训练无关的注意力引导，PanoPlane有效解决了稀疏视角下室内场景新视角合成中的几何一致性问题，验证了全景先验在3D重建中的强大作用。

Abstract: We present PanoPlane, an approach for high-fidelity sparse-view indoor novel view synthesis that reconstructs closed room geometry via panoramic scene completion. Unlike perspective-based methods that generate training views from limited fields of view, PanoPlane leverages $360^{\circ}$ panoramic completion to condition the generative process on the full spatial layout. We propose Layout Anchored Attention Steering, a training-free mechanism that steers attention within the diffusion model's internal representation toward scene's detected planar surfaces at inference time. By directing each unobserved region's attention toward geometrically consistent observed content, our method replaces unconstrained hallucination with grounded surface extrapolation. The resulting panoramic completions provide supervision for 3D Gaussian Splatting, enabling accurate novel-view synthesis across unobserved regions from as few as three input views. Experiments on Replica, ScanNet++, and Matterport3D demonstrate state-of-the-art novel view synthesis quality across 3, 6, and 9 input views, achieving up to $+17.8\%$ improvement in PSNR over the current state-of-the-art baseline without any training or fine-tuning of the diffusion model.

</details>


### [15] [Denoising-GS: Gaussian Splatting with Spatial-aware Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14880)
*Qingyuan Zhou,Xinyi Liu,Weidong Yang,Ning Wang,Shuquan Ye,Ben Fei,Ying He,Wanli Ouyang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Denoising-GS，将3D高斯溅射优化视为原始去噪过程，通过空间感知去噪框架（包括空间梯度去噪、不确定性去噪和空间一致性细化）来减少噪声高斯原语，在三个基准数据集上实现了最先进的新视图合成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS优化仅调整原语位置而忽略空间结构，且从SfM点云初始化稀疏不完整，导致引入噪声高斯原语。

Method: 1) 空间优化流保持的原语去噪优化器；2) 空间梯度去噪策略，联合考虑原语空间支持以实现梯度一致更新；3) 不确定性去噪模块，估算原语级不确定性以修剪冗余噪声原语；4) 空间一致性细化策略，在稀疏区域选择性分裂原语以保持结构完整性。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上，Denoising-GS一致提升了NVS保真度，同时保持了表示紧凑性，达到了最先进性能。

Conclusion: 将3DGS优化建模为原语去噪过程，并引入空间感知去噪框架，有效提升了渲染质量和模型紧凑性。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have achieved remarkable success in high-fidelity Novel View Synthesis (NVS), yet the optimization process inevitably introduces noisy Gaussian primitives due to the sparse and incomplete initialization from Structure-from-Motion (SfM) point clouds. Most existing methods focus solely on adjusting the positions of primitives during optimization, while neglecting the underlying spatial structure. To this end, we introduce a new perspective by formulating the optimization of 3DGS as a primitive denoising process and propose Denoising-GS, a spatial-aware denoising framework for Gaussian primitives by taking both the positions and spatial structure into consideration. Specifically, we design an optimizer that preserves the spatial optimization flow of primitives, facilitating coherent and directed denoising rather than random perturbations. Building upon this, the Spatial Gradient-based Denoising strategy jointly considers the spatial supports of primitives to ensure gradient-consistent updates. Furthermore, the Uncertainty-based Denoising module estimates primitive-wise uncertainty to prune redundant or noisy primitives, while the Spatial Coherence Refinement strategy selectively splits primitives in sparse regions to maintain structural completeness. Experiments conducted on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that Denoising-GS consistently enhances NVS fidelity while maintaining representation compactness, achieving state-of-the-art performance across all benchmarks. Source code and models will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [16] [TeDiO: Temporal Diagonal Optimization for Training-Free Coherent Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14136)
*Nurislam Tursynbek,Zhiqiang Lao,Heather Yu,Gedas Bertasius,Marc Niethammer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TeDiO是一种无需训练、推理时的方法，通过正则化中间自注意力图中的时间对角线模式来提升文本到视频扩散模型的时序一致性，有效减少闪烁、漂移等不稳定运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到视频扩散Transformer生成的帧在视觉上令人满意，但时序一致性差，常出现闪烁、漂移和不稳定运动，亟需一种无需额外训练或外部运动监督的轻量级解决方案。

Method: 观察到不连贯视频的自注意力图中存在不规则、碎片化的时间对角线，而稳定运动对应平滑的带状对角线模式；TeDiO据此估计对角线平滑度，识别不稳定区域，并进行轻量级潜在特征更新以促进帧间一致动态。

Result: 在Wan2.1、CogVideoX等多种视频扩散模型上，TeDiO实现了显著更平滑的运动，同时保持单帧视觉质量。

Conclusion: TeDiO作为一种高效即插即用的方法，无需修改模型权重或外部运动监督，即可有效提升现代视频生成系统的动态真实感。

Abstract: Recent text-to-video diffusion transformers generate visually compelling frames, yet still struggle with temporal coherence, often producing flickering, drifting, or unstable motion. We show that these failures leave a clear imprint inside the model: incoherent videos consistently exhibit irregular, fragmented temporal diagonals in their intermediate self-attention maps, whereas stable motion corresponds to smooth, band-diagonal patterns. Building on this observation, we introduce TeDiO, a training-free, inference-time method that reinforces temporal consistency by regularizing these internal attention patterns. TeDiO estimates diagonal smoothness, identifies unstable regions, and performs lightweight latent updates that promote coherent frame-to-frame dynamics, without modifying model weights or using external motion supervision. Across multiple video diffusion models (e.g., Wan2.1, CogVideoX), TeDiO delivers markedly smoother motion while preserving per-frame visual quality, offering an efficient plug-and-play approach to improving dynamic realism in modern video generation systems.

</details>


### [17] [Rethinking the Good Enough Embedding for Easy Few-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14145)
*Michael Karnes,Alper Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文证明了无需微调的冻结DINOv2-L特征结合k-NN分类器即可在少样本学习中超越复杂的元学习算法，支持了"通用嵌入足以应对复杂任务"的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在深度视觉识别向通用表征范式转变的背景下，Platonic Representation Hypothesis指出不同架构正收敛到共享的"理想"隐空间。这引出一个关键问题：好的嵌入是否足以解决复杂任务？本文旨在验证无需任务特定微调，现成的嵌入即可胜任。

Method: 提出一个完全无需反向传播的非参数化流水线：使用冻结的DINOv2-L特征，通过逐层表征分析确定最优特征提取层，采用k-Nearest Neighbor分类器，并通过PCA和ICA进行流形精炼以提供正则化效果。

Result: 在四个主流少样本学习基准上，该方法持续超越了复杂的元学习算法，达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 验证了"Good Embedding All You Need"的假设，表明在现代大规模预训练模型下，冻结的通用嵌入配合简单的非参数化方法足以在少样本学习中取得SOTA性能，无需繁重的任务特定微调。

Abstract: The field of deep visual recognition is undergoing a paradigm shift toward universal representations. The Platonic Representation Hypothesis suggests that diverse architectures trained on massive datasets are converging toward a shared, "ideal" latent space. This again raises a critical question: is a "Good Embedding All You Need?" In this paper, we leverage this convergence to demonstrate that off-the-shelf embeddings are inherently "good enough" for complex tasks, rendering intensive task-specific fine-tuning unnecessary. We explore this hypothesis within the few-shot learning framework, proposing a straightforward, non-parametric pipeline that entirely bypasses backpropagation. By utilizing a k-Nearest Neighbor classifier on frozen DINOv2-L features, we conduct a layer-wise characterization to identify an optimal feature extraction. We further demonstrate that manifold refinement via PCA and ICA provides a beneficial regularizing effect. Our results across four major benchmarks demonstrate that our approach consistently surpasses sophisticated meta-learning algorithms, achieving state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [18] [CoReDiT: Spatial Coherence-Guided Token Pruning and Reconstruction for Efficient Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14191)
*Zhuojin Li,Hsin-Pai Cheng,Hong Cai,Shizhong Han,Fatih Porikli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoReDiT提出了一种基于空间相干性的结构化令牌剪枝框架，通过线性时间相干性评分识别并跳过冗余令牌，并利用相干性引导的聚合重建被跳过的注意力输出，在多种DiT模型上实现了高达55%的自注意力FLOPs降低和1.33倍（云GPU）至1.72倍（移动NPU）的推理加速，同时保持高视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs)在图像和视频生成中表现出色，但计算成本高昂，限制了其可扩展性和设备端部署。现有方法缺乏针对DiT的结构化剪枝框架，难以在保持生成质量的同时实现高效推理。

Method: 1. 线性时间空间相干性评分：估计潜在令牌格中的局部冗余性。2. 高相干性令牌跳过：在自注意力中跳过冗余令牌。3. 相干性引导的聚合重建：利用邻近保留令牌的注意力输出重建被跳过令牌的表示，避免视觉不连续性。4. 渐进式、块自适应剪枝调度：逐步增加剪枝力度，为冗余更高的块和去噪步分配更大的剪枝预算。

Result: 在PixArt-α和MagicDrive-V2等最先进扩散骨干上：自注意力FLOPs降低高达55%；云GPU上推理加速1.33倍；移动NPU上推理加速1.72倍；增加设备端内存余量，支持更高分辨率生成。

Conclusion: CoReDiT是一个高效的结构化令牌剪枝框架，能在显著降低DiT计算开销的同时保持生成质量，有助于DiT在资源受限设备上的部署和高分辨率生成。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) deliver remarkable image and video generation quality but incur high computational cost, limiting scalability and on-device deployment. We introduce CoReDiT, a structured token pruning framework for DiTs across vision tasks. CoReDiT uses a linear-time spatial coherence score to estimate local redundancy in the latent token lattice and skips high coherence (redundant) tokens in self-attention. To maintain a dense representation and avoid visual discontinuities, we reconstruct skipped attention outputs via coherence-guided aggregation of spatially neighboring retained tokens. We further introduce a progressive, block-adaptive pruning schedule that increases pruning gradually and allocates larger budgets to blocks and denoising steps with higher redundancy. Across state-of-the-art diffusion backbones including PixArt-α and MagicDrive-V2, CoReDiT achieves up to 55% self-attention FLOPs reduction and inference speedups of 1.33x on cloud GPUs and 1.72x on mobile NPUs, while maintaining high visual quality. Notably, CoReDiT also increases on-device memory head-room, enabling higher-resolution generation.

</details>


### [19] [Towards Real-Time Autonomous Navigation: Transformer-Based Catheter Tip Tracking in Fluoroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14253)
*Harry Robertshaw,Yanghe Hao,Weiyuan Deng,Benjamin Jackson,S. M. Hadi Sadati,Nikola Fischer,Tom Vercauteren,Alejandro Granados,Thomas C. Booth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多线程实时导管尖端跟踪流水线，结合深度学习分割模型（SegFormer最优）和后处理技术，在低对比度、噪声和遮挡的透视图像下实现高精度跟踪，为基于强化学习的机械血栓切除术自主导航提供基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械血栓切除术（MT）可改善中风预后，但受限于本地治疗可及性。强化学习（RL）机器人系统可通过自主导航缓解此问题，但现有RL方法需要实时设备尖端坐标跟踪，而当前在透视图像下跟踪存在低对比度、噪声和器械遮挡等挑战。

Method: 设计多线程流水线，包含帧读取、预处理、推理和后处理模块。训练并比较U-Net、U-Net+Transformer和SegFormer等深度学习分割模型（二分类和三分类）。后处理采用两步组件滤波、单像素中轴骨架化、贪心弧长路径跟踪及轮廓回退策略。

Result: 在中等复杂度透视视频数据上，二分类SegFormer平均绝对误差为4.44 mm，优于U-Net（4.60 mm）、U-Net+Transformer（6.20 mm）及所有三分类模型（5.19-7.74 mm）。在分割基准上，该方法在三分割任务中Dice分数较CathAction提升最高5%。

Conclusion: 所提出的多线程跟踪框架在挑战性成像条件下保持稳定性能，超越先前基准，为基于RL的自主MT导航提供了可靠且高效的基础。

Abstract: Purpose: Mechanical thrombectomy (MT) improves stroke outcomes, but is limited by a lack of local treatment access. Widespread distribution of reinforcement learning (RL)-based robotic systems can be used to alleviate this challenge through autonomous navigation, but current RL methods require live device tip coordinate tracking to function. This paper aims to develop and evaluate a real-time catheter tip tracking pipeline under fluoroscopy, addressing challenges such as low contrast, noise, and device occlusion. Methods: A multi-threaded pipeline was designed, incorporating frame reading, preprocessing, inference, and post-processing. Deep learning segmentation models, including U-Net, U-Net+Transformer, and SegFormer, were trained and benchmarked using two-class and three-class formulations. Post-processing involved two-step component filtering, one-pixel medial skeletonization, and greedy arc-length path following with contour fall-back. Results: On manually-labeled moderate complexity fluoroscopic video data, the two-class SegFormer achieved a mean absolute error of 4.44 mm, outperforming U-Net (4.60 mm), U-Net+Transformer (6.20 mm) and all three-class models (5.19-7.74 mm). On segmentation benchmarks, the system exceeded state-of-the-art CathAction results with improvements of up to +5% in Dice scores for three-segmentation. Conclusion: The results demonstrate that the proposed multi-threaded tracking framework maintains stable performance under challenging imaging conditions, outperforming prior benchmarks, while providing a reliable and efficient foundation for RL-based autonomous MT navigation.

</details>


### [20] [Image Restoration via Diffusion Models with Dynamic Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14267)
*Yang Zheng,Wen Li,Zhaoqiang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于动态分辨率扩散模型的子空间投影方法（SubDPS/SubDAPS/SubDAPS++），通过将数据投影到低维子空间来加速扩散模型在图像恢复任务中的推理过程，在多数实验场景下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在图像恢复任务中计算开销大：像素空间方法直接在高维像素空间操作导致高计算量；潜在空间方法虽用VAE压缩但需反复编解码推理，实际运行效率甚至低于像素空间方法。旨在解决这种计算低效问题。

Method: 提出动态分辨率扩散模型，将数据投影到低维子空间以加速推理。首先对预训练扩散模型进行微调以获得动态分辨率先验，然后将两种像素空间方法（DPS和DAPS）适配到该框架中，分别得到SubDPS和SubDAPS。进一步提出增强变体SubDAPS++以提升重建效率和质量。

Result: 在多种图像数据集和不同恢复任务上的实验表明，所提方法在大多数实验场景中优于近期基于扩散模型的方法。

Conclusion: 通过子空间投影结合动态分辨率扩散模型，有效降低了计算开销并保持了重建质量，为扩散模型在图像恢复中的高效推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Diffusion models (DMs) have exhibited remarkable efficacy in various image restoration tasks. However, existing approaches typically operate within the high-dimensional pixel space, resulting in high computational overhead. While methods based on latent DMs seek to alleviate this issue by utilizing the compressed latent space of a variational autoencoder, they require repeated encoder-decoder inference. This introduces significant additional computational burdens, often resulting in runtime performance that is even inferior to that of their pixel-space counterparts. To mitigate the computational inefficiency, this work proposes projecting data into lower-dimensional subspaces using dynamic resolution DMs to accelerate the inference process. We first fine-tune pre-trained DMs for dynamic resolution priors and adapt DPS and DAPS, which are two widely used pixel-space methods for general image restoration tasks, into the proposed framework, yielding methods we refer to as SubDPS and SubDAPS, respectively. Given the favorable inference speed and reconstruction fidelity of SubDAPS, we introduce an enhanced variant termed SubDAPS++ to further boost both reconstruction efficiency and quality. Empirical evaluations across diverse image datasets and various restoration tasks demonstrate that the proposed methods outperform recent DM-based approaches in the majority of experimental scenarios. The code is available at https://github.com/StarNextDay/SubDAPS.git.

</details>


### [21] [Diagnosing and Correcting Concept Omission in Multimodal Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14270)
*Kanghyun Baek,Jaihyun Lew,Chaehun Shin,Jungbeom Lee,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现多模态扩散Transformer（MM-DiTs）在文本到图像生成中存在概念遗漏问题，通过线性探测发现文本嵌入中存在表征目标概念缺失的“遗漏信号”，并提出遗漏信号干预（OSI）方法，通过放大该信号主动催化缺失概念的生成，在FLUX.1-Dev和SD3.5-Medium上显著缓解了概念遗漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态扩散Transformer在文本到图像生成中频繁出现概念遗漏问题，即指定的对象或属性未能在生成图像中出现，需要有效缓解该问题。

Method: 通过线性探测（linear probing）文本token，发现文本嵌入中存在表征目标概念缺失的“遗漏信号”（omission signal）；提出OSI方法，通过放大遗漏信号来主动催化缺失概念的生成。

Result: 在FLUX.1-Dev和SD3.5-Medium上，OSI显著缓解了概念遗漏，即使在极端场景下也有效。

Conclusion: 通过放大文本嵌入中的遗漏信号，可以有效缓解MM-DiTs在文本到图像生成中的概念遗漏问题。

Abstract: Multimodal Diffusion Transformers (MM-DiTs) have achieved remarkable progress in text-to-image generation, yet they frequently suffer from concept omission, where specified objects or attributes fail to emerge in the generated image. By performing linear probing on text tokens, we demonstrate that text embeddings can distinguish a characteristic `omission signal' representing the absence of target concepts. Leveraging this insight, we propose Omission Signal Intervention (OSI), which amplifies the omission signal to actively catalyze the generation of missing concepts. Comprehensive experiments on FLUX.1-Dev and SD3.5-Medium demonstrate that OSI significantly alleviates concept omission even in extreme scenarios.

</details>


### [22] [CreFlow: Corrective Reflow for Sparse-Reward Embodied Video Diffusion RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14274)
*Zhenyang Ni,Yijiang Li,Ruochen Jiao,Simon Sinong Zhan,Sipeng Chen,Zhenfei Yin,Minshuo Chen,Philip Torr,Zhaoran Wang,Qi Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CreFlow框架，通过组合线性时序逻辑约束的奖励模型和在线强化学习后训练，有效提升视频生成模型在具身操作任务中的物理合理性，实验显示下游执行成功率提升23.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型在具身操作中会产生违反物理约束的视觉结果，而现有视频强化学习奖励将视频简化为低级视觉指标，无法满足操作任务中基于逻辑验证的组合式任务规范评估需求。

Method: 提出组合约束奖励模型，自动将任务需求形式化为线性时序逻辑约束的组合；在此基础上提出CreFlow在线RL框架，包含两个关键设计：1）信用感知的NFT损失，将RL更新限制在奖励相关区域；2）修正重流损失，利用组内正样本作为修正方向的显式估计。

Result: CreFlow的奖励判断与人类及模拟器成功标签的一致性优于现有方法，在八项双臂操作任务中下游执行成功率提升23.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 基于逻辑约束的奖励建模和针对性在线RL后训练能有效提升视频生成模型在具身操作任务中的物理合理性，为视频生成与机器人操作的结合提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Video generation models trained on heterogeneous data with likelihood-surrogate objectives can produce visually plausible rollouts that violate physical constraints in embodied manipulation. Although reinforcement-learning post-training offers a natural route to adapting VGMs, existing video-RL rewards often reduce each rollout to a low-level visual metric, whereas manipulation video evaluation requires logic-based verification of whether the rollout satisfies a compositional task specification. To fill this gap, we introduce a compositional constraint-based reward model for post-training embodied video generation models, which automatically formulates task requirements as a composition of Linear Temporal Logic constraints, providing faithful rewards and localized error information in generated videos. To achieve effective improvement in high-dimensional video generation using these reward signals, we further propose CreFlow, a novel online RL framework with two key designs: i) a credit-aware NFT loss that confines the RL update to reward-relevant regions, preventing perturbations to unrelated regions during post-training; and ii) a corrective reflow loss that leverages within-group positive samples as an explicit estimate of the correction direction, stabilizing and accelerating training. Experiments show that CreFlow yields reward judgments better aligned with human and simulator success labels than existing methods and improves downstream execution success by 23.8 percentage points across eight bimanual manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [23] [ICED: Concept-level Machine Unlearning via Interpretable Concept Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14309)
*Shen Lin,Jing Lin,Junhao Dong,Piotr Koniusz,Li Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种可解释的概念级机器遗忘框架，通过多模态大语言模型构建任务特定概念词汇表，将视觉表示分解为稀疏非负的概念组合，在VLM中实现精准的目标概念遗忘，同时保留同一图像中的非目标语义和全局跨模态知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM遗忘方法在图像或实例层面操作，难以精确移除目标知识而不影响无关语义，因为单张图像常包含多个纠缠的概念（既包括要遗忘的目标概念，也包括应保留的上下文信息）。

Method: 1) 利用多模态大语言模型从遗忘集构建紧凑的任务特定概念词汇表；2) 进行模态对齐；3) 将视觉表示分解为稀疏非负的概念组合；4) 将遗忘形式化为概念级优化，选择性抑制目标概念，同时保留实例内非目标语义和全局跨模态知识。

Result: 在域内和域外遗忘设置下，该方法实现了更全面的目标遗忘，更好地保留同一图像中的非目标知识，并保持了与现有VLM遗忘方法相当模型效用。

Conclusion: 概念级可解释遗忘框架能有效解决VLM中细粒度知识移除的难题，在遗忘精度和知识保留之间取得了更好的平衡。

Abstract: Machine unlearning in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) is typically performed at the image or instance level, making it difficult to precisely remove target knowledge without affecting unrelated semantics. This issue is especially pronounced since a single image often contains multiple entangled concepts, including both target concepts to be forgotten and contextual information that should be preserved. In this paper, we propose an interpretable concept-level unlearning framework for VLMs, which constructs a compact task-specific concept vocabulary from the forgetting set using a multimodal large language model. In addition to modality alignment, visual representations are decomposed into sparse, nonnegative combinations of semantic concepts, providing an explicit interface for fine-grained knowledge manipulation. Based on this decomposition, our method formulates unlearning as concept-level optimization, where target concepts are selectively suppressed while intra-instance non-target semantics and global cross-modal knowledge are preserved. Extensive experiments across both in-domain and out-of-domain forgetting settings demonstrate that our method enables more comprehensive target forgetting, better preserves non-target knowledge within the same image, and maintains competitive model utility compared with existing VLM unlearning methods.

</details>


### [24] [CoRDS: Coreset-based Representative and Diverse Selection for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14310)
*Ailar Mahdizadeh,Puria Azadi,Muchen Li,Xiangteng He,Leonid Sigal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将流式视频理解中的KV缓存压缩视为核心集选择问题，通过联合键值表示中的双目标优化（覆盖关键空间和值空间）及正交性驱动的多样性准则，在固定缓存预算下优于传统基于启发式（如时效性、冗余性、显著性）的逐token剪枝方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频理解方法压缩KV缓存时依赖局部逐token启发式规则（如时效性、时序冗余、显著性），未能显式优化保留缓存是否真正代表累积历史的整体几何结构，导致信息损失。

Method: 1) 将KV缓存压缩建模为核心集选择问题，而非独立评分剪枝；2) 在联合KV表示空间中引入双目标优化，平衡关键空间（检索结构）和值空间（输出相关信息）的覆盖；3) 引入正交性驱动的多样性准则，鼓励选择贡献新方向的候选，并与对数行列式子集选择建立联系。

Result: 在四种开源VLM和五个长视频/流式视频基准上，在固定缓存预算下，所提方法优于启发式流式压缩基线。

Conclusion: 代表性核心集选择比逐token剪枝更有效，为内存受限的流式视频理解提供了更优原则。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding with large vision-language models (VLMs) requires a compact memory that can support future reasoning over an ever-growing visual history. A common solution is to compress the key-value (KV) cache, but existing streaming methods typically rely on local token-wise heuristics, such as recency, temporal redundancy, or saliency, which do not explicitly optimize whether the retained cache is representative of the accumulated history. We propose to view KV-cache compression as a coreset selection problem: rather than scoring tokens independently for retention, we select a small subset that covers the geometry of the accumulated visual cache. Our method operates in a joint KV representation and introduces a bicriteria objective that balances coverage in key and value spaces, preserving both retrieval structure and output-relevant information. To encourage a more diverse retained subset, we further introduce an orthogonality-driven diversity criterion that favors candidates contributing new directions beyond the current selection, and connect this criterion to log-determinant subset selection. Across four open-source VLMs and five long-video and streaming-video benchmarks, our method improves over heuristic streaming compression baselines under a fixed cache budget. These results highlight that representative coreset selection offers a more effective principle, than token-wise pruning, for memory-constrained streaming video understanding.

</details>


### [25] [TurboVGGT: Fast Visual Geometry Reconstruction with Adaptive Alternating Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14315)
*David Huang,Guile Wu,Chengjie Huang,Bingbing Liu,Dongfeng Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TurboVGGT提出了一种高效的视觉几何变换器，通过自适应交替注意力机制（包括自适应稀疏全局注意力和帧内注意力）实现快速多视图3D重建，在保持竞争性重建质量的同时显著提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈式3D重建方法难以在重建质量和计算效率之间取得平衡；高效视觉几何变换器通常跨层和跨帧使用相同的稀疏比率，缺乏自适应学习代表性token以捕捉全局关系的能力，导致性能次优。

Method: 基于高效视觉几何变换器，提出端到端可训练框架：1) 自适应稀疏全局注意力（通过自适应稀疏选择学习不同稀疏级别的代表性token以建模全局几何关系）；2) 帧注意力（聚合每帧内的局部细节）。自适应机制考虑了token重要性随帧变化、注意力层在不同抽象级别操作、全局依赖依赖于结构信息丰富区域等因素。

Result: 在多个3D重建基准上，TurboVGGT实现了快速多视图重建，同时与最先进方法相比保持了具有竞争力的重建质量。

Conclusion: TurboVGGT通过自适应交替注意力机制有效平衡了多视图3D重建中的质量和效率，验证了自适应稀疏策略在全局几何建模中的有效性。

Abstract: Recent feed-forward 3D reconstruction methods, such as visual geometry transformers, have substantially advanced the traditional per-scene optimization paradigm by enabling effective multi-view reconstruction in a single forward pass. However, most existing methods struggle to achieve a balance between reconstruction quality and computational efficiency, which limits their scalability and efficiency. Although some efficient visual geometry transformers have recently emerged, they typically use the same sparsity ratio across layers and frames and lack mechanisms to adaptively learn representative tokens to capture global relationships, leading to suboptimal performance. In this work, we propose TurboVGGT, a novel approach that employs an efficient visual geometry transformer with adaptive alternating attention for fast multi-view 3D reconstruction. Specifically, TurboVGGT employs an end-to-end trainable framework with adaptive sparse global attention guided by adaptive sparsity selection to capture global relationships across frames and frame attention to aggregate local details within each frame. In the adaptive sparse global attention, TurboVGGT adaptively learns representative tokens with varying sparsity levels for global geometry modeling, considering that token importance varies across frames, attention layers operate tokens at different levels of abstraction, and global dependencies rely on structurally informative regions. Extensive experiments on multiple 3D reconstruction benchmarks demonstrate that TurboVGGT achieves fast multi-view reconstruction while maintaining competitive reconstruction quality compared with state-of-the-art methods. Project page: https://turbovggt.github.io/.

</details>


### [26] [D2-CDIG: Controlled Diffusion Remote Sensing Image Generation with Dual Priors of DEM and Cloud-Fog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14326)
*Zuopeng Zhao,Ying Liu,Kanyaphakphachsorn Pharksuwan,Su Luo,Xiaoyu Li,Maocai Ning*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出D2-CDIG框架，利用扩散模型结合DEM和云-雾信息作为双先验控制，通过地面和大气分支解耦生成过程，实现高精度、高真实感的遥感图像生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控遥感图像生成方法依赖分割、边缘检测等传统技术，未充分利用地形和大气条件信息，导致复杂地形和大气现象下生成的图像缺乏准确性和自然性。

Method: 提出D2-CDIG框架，集成扩散模型与双先验控制机制：1）将DEM和云-雾信息作为双先验知识；2）通过独立控制的地面分支和大气分支解耦地形与大气生成过程；3）引入精细化云-雾滑块灵活调整云层厚度与分布；4）在训练中分层注入地面和大气控制信号以确保图像无缝过渡。

Result: 与传统基于分割或边缘检测的方法相比，D2-CDIG在图像质量、细节丰富度和真实感方面有显著提升。

Conclusion: D2-CDIG为遥感图像生成提供了一种灵活且精确的解决方案，可为遥感大模型及下游任务提供高质量数据。

Abstract: Remote sensing image generation provides a reliable data foundation for remote sensing large models and downstream tasks. However, existing controllable remote sensing image generation methods typically rely on traditional techniques such as segmentation and edge detection, which do not fully leverage terrain or atmospheric conditions. As a result, the generated images often lack accuracy and naturalness when dealing with complex terrains and atmospheric phenomena. In this paper, we propose a novel remote sensing image generation framework, D2-CDIG, which integrates diffusion models with a dual-prior control mechanism. By incorporating both Digital Elevation Model (DEM) and cloud-fog information as dual prior knowledge, D2-CDIG precisely controls ground features and atmospheric phenomena within the generated images. Specifically, D2-CDIG decouples the terrain and atmospheric generation processes through independent control of ground and atmospheric branches. Additionally, a refined cloud-fog slider is introduced to flexibly adjust cloud thickness and distribution. During training, ground and atmospheric control signals are injected in layers to ensure a seamless transition within the images. Compared to traditional methods based on segmentation or edge detection, D2-CDIG shows significant improvements in image quality, detail richness, and realism. D2-CDIG offers a flexible and precise solution for remote sensing image generation, providing high-quality data for training large remote sensing models and downstream tasks.

</details>


### [27] [InsightTok: Improving Text and Face Fidelity in Discrete Tokenization for Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14333)
*Yang Yue,Fangyun Wei,Tianyu He,Jinjing Zhao,Zanlin Ni,Zeyu Liu,Jiayi Guo,Lei Shi,Yue Dong,Li Chen,Ji Li,Gao Huang,Dong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InsightTok，一种通过局部化、内容感知的感知损失增强文本和面部重建质量的离散视觉分词框架，在16倍下采样率和小型码本下显著提升文本可读性和面部保真度，并有效迁移至自回归图像生成任务InsightAR中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于离散分词的自回归生成器在文本和面部等细节丰富的模式上表现不佳，根本原因在于标准离散分词器的目标函数与文本可读性和面部保真度弱对齐，通常只优化通用重建而均匀压缩多样化内容。

Method: 提出InsightTok框架，采用局部化、内容感知的感知损失作为监督信号，在16倍下采样率和16k码本条件下训练分词器，针对性强化文本和面部的细粒度结构保留。

Result: InsightTok在文本和面部重建任务上显著优于此前分词器，且不牺牲通用重建质量；该增益一致地迁移至自回归图像生成模型InsightAR中，生成更清晰的文本和更忠实的面部细节。

Conclusion: 在分词器训练中引入专门的感知监督能够有效推动离散图像生成的发展，为提升文本和面部等关键模式的生成质量提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Text and faces are among the most perceptually salient and practically important patterns in visual generation, yet they remain challenging for autoregressive generators built on discrete tokenization. A central bottleneck is the tokenizer: aggressive downsampling and quantization often discard the fine-grained structures needed to preserve readable glyphs and distinctive facial features. We attribute this gap to standard discrete-tokenizer objectives being weakly aligned with text legibility and facial fidelity, as these objectives typically optimize generic reconstruction while compressing diverse content uniformly. To address this, we propose InsightTok, a simple yet effective discrete visual tokenization framework that enhances text and face fidelity through localized, content-aware perceptual losses. With a compact 16k codebook and a 16x downsampling rate, InsightTok significantly outperforms prior tokenizers in text and face reconstruction without compromising general reconstruction quality. These gains consistently transfer to autoregressive image generation in InsightAR, producing images with clearer text and more faithful facial details. Overall, our results highlight the potential of specialized supervision in tokenizer training for advancing discrete image generation.

</details>


### [28] [IG-Diff: Complex Night Scene Restoration with Illumination-Guided Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14337)
*Yifan Chen,Fei Yin,Chunle Guo,Chongyi Li,Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对夜间复杂场景（同时存在低光照和天气退化）的恢复问题，提出了光照引导的扩散模型，并构建了模拟多种退化的夜间数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像恢复方法只能处理单一退化形式，无法应对夜间复杂场景中低光照与天气退化共存的情况；同时缺乏包含多种退化形式的配对数据集，阻碍了端到端解决方案的发展。

Method: 构建了模拟低光照与其他退化形式共存的复杂夜间场景数据集；提出将光照引导模块嵌入扩散模型，以指导光照恢复过程。

Result: 该方法能够在低光照场景下应对多种退化带来的挑战，同时保持纹理保真度。

Conclusion: 光照引导的扩散模型能有效解决夜间复杂多退化场景的图像恢复问题，在纹理保真和抗退化方面表现良好。

Abstract: In nighttime circumstances, it is challenging for individuals and machines to perceive their surroundings. While prevailing image restoration methods adeptly handle singular forms of degradation, they falter when confronted with intricate nocturnal scenes, such as the concurrent presence of weather and low-light conditions. Compounding this challenge, the lack of paired data that encapsulates the coexistence of low-light situations and other forms of degradation hinders the development of a comprehensive end-to-end solution. In this work, we contribute complex nighttime scene datasets that simulate both illumination degradation and other forms of deterioration. To address the complexity of night degradation, we propose an integration of an illumination-guided module embedded in the diffusion model to guide the illumination restoration process. Our model can preserve texture fidelity while contending with the adversities posed by various degradation in low-light scenarios.

</details>


### [29] [AnyBand-Diff: A Unified Remote Sensing Image Generation and Band Repair Framework with Spectral Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14341)
*Zuopeng Zhao,Ying Liu,Xiaoyu Li,Su Luo,Lu Li,Wenwen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AnyBand-Diff，一种基于光谱先验引导的扩散框架，用于遥感图像的鲁棒光谱重建。该方法通过掩码条件扩散主干、物理引导采样机制和多尺度物理损失，解决了现有扩散模型在遥感图像中产生的光谱失真和辐射不一致问题，实验证明了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在生成真实图像方面取得了显著进展，但直接应用于遥感图像时忽略了固有的物理规律，导致光谱失真和辐射不一致，严重限制了生成数据的科学实用性。

Method: 1) 设计掩码条件扩散主干，结合双随机掩码策略，从任意波段子集中恢复完整光谱信息；2) 提出物理引导采样机制，利用可微分物理模型的梯度将去噪轨迹引导至物理合理解流形；3) 构建多尺度物理损失，在像素、区域和全局层面施加联合约束。

Result: 大量实验证实AnyBand-Diff能够生成可靠的遥感图像，实现准确的光谱重建，推进了地球观测中物理感知生成方法的发展。

Conclusion: AnyBand-Diff通过融合物理先验与扩散模型，有效解决了遥感图像生成中的光谱与辐射一致性问题，为物理感知的生成式遥感方法做出了贡献。

Abstract: Existing diffusion models have made significant progress in generating realistic images. However, their direct adaptation to remote sensing imagery often disregards intrinsic physical laws. This oversight frequently leads to spectral distortion and radiometric inconsistency, severely limiting the scientific utility of generated data. To address this issue, this paper introduces AnyBand-Diff, a novel spectral-prior-guided diffusion framework tailored for robust spectral reconstruction. Specifically, we design a Masked Conditional Diffusion backbone integrated with a dual stochastic masking strategy, empowering the model to recover complete spectral information from arbitrary band subsets. Subsequently, to ensure radiometric fidelity, a Physics-Guided Sampling mechanism is proposed, leveraging gradients from a differentiable physical model to explicitly steer the denoising trajectory toward the manifold of physically plausible solutions. Furthermore, a Multi-Scale Physical Loss is formulated to enforce rigorous constraints across pixel, region, and global levels in a joint manner. Extensive experiments confirm the effectiveness of AnyBand-Diff in generating reliable imagery and achieving accurate spectral reconstruction, contributing to the advancement of physics-aware generative methods for Earth observation.

</details>


### [30] [Dual-Latent Collaborative Decoding for Fidelity-Perception Balanced Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14391)
*Qi Mao,Zijian Wang,Zhengxue Cheng,Lingyu Zhu,Siwei Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MoDE（混合解码专家），一种双潜变量协作解码框架，通过将SQ分支作为保真度专家、VQ分支作为感知专家，并借助专家特定增强（ESE）和跨专家调制（CEM）两个解码端模块实现协同，在宽比特率范围内实现保真度与感知质量更优的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习图像压缩方法大多依赖单一压缩潜变量同时承载结构细节、语义线索和感知先验，导致不同潜变量范式（SQ连续潜变量和VQ离散标记）在保真度与感知质量之间存在固有矛盾：SQ在低码率下丢失感知细节，VQ则结构保真度和码率可扩展性有限。

Method: MoDE提出双潜变量协作解码框架：(1) 将SQ分支作为保真度导向专家，VQ分支作为感知导向专家；(2) 设计两个解码端模块：专家特定增强（ESE）保留分支特定专家参考，跨专家调制（CEM）实现选择性互补信息传递；(3) 支持共享双流比特流下的保真度锚定和感知锚定两种解码模式。

Result: 在宽比特率范围内，MoDE在保真度-感知平衡上优于代表性的失真导向、感知导向、生成式和双潜变量基线方法。

Conclusion: 解码端专家协作被证明是实现宽范围保真度-感知平衡LIC的有效设计范式。

Abstract: Learned image compression (LIC) increasingly requires reconstructions that balance distortion fidelity and perceptual realism across a wide range of bitrates. However, most existing methods still rely on a single compressed latent representation to simultaneously carry structural details, semantic cues, and perceptual priors, requiring the same latent representation to serve multiple, potentially conflicting roles. This tension becomes evident across different latent paradigms: scalar-quantized (SQ) continuous latents provide rate-scalable fidelity but tend to lose perceptual details at low rates, while vector-quantized (VQ) discrete tokens preserve compact semantic cues but suffer from limited structural fidelity and bitrate scalability. To address this issue, we propose Mixture of Decoder Experts (MoDE), a dual-latent collaborative decoding framework that decomposes reconstruction responsibilities across complementary latent paradigms. Specifically, MoDE treats the SQ branch as a fidelity-oriented expert and the VQ branch as a perception-oriented expert, and coordinates them through two decoder-side modules: Expert-Specific Enhancement (ESE), which preserves branch-specific expert references, and Cross-Expert Modulation (CEM), which enables selective complementary transfer during reconstruction. The resulting framework supports selective cross-latent collaboration under a shared dual-stream bitstream and enables both fidelity-anchored and perception-anchored decoding. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MoDE achieves a more favorable fidelity-perception balance than representative distortion-oriented, perception-oriented, generative, and dual-latent baselines across a wide bitrate range, highlighting decoder-side expert collaboration as an effective design for wide-range fidelity-perception balanced LIC.

</details>


### [31] [Systematic Discovery of Semantic Attacks in Online Map Construction through Conditional Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14396)
*Chenyi Wang,Ruoyu Song,Raymond Muller,Jean-Philippe Monteuuis,Jonathan Petit,Z. Berkay Celik,Ryan Gerdes,Ming F. Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MIRAGE是一个利用扩散模型潜在流形发现语义攻击（如阴影、湿滑路面）的框架，可绕过对抗防御，通过边界移除（抑制57.7%检测，破坏96%轨迹规划）和边界注入（唯一成功注入虚假边界的方法）攻击在线HD地图构建，揭示了语义级扰动比像素级扰动难以防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有像素级扰动攻击可被标准对抗防御中和，亟需发现能绕过防御的语义级攻击方法，揭示自动驾驶HD地图构建的安全漏洞。

Method: 利用扩散模型学习的真实数据潜在流形，搜索与真实场景具有相同道路拓扑但能误导地图预测的语义变异场景（如阴影、湿路面）。

Result: 边界移除攻击抑制57.7%检测，破坏96%规划轨迹；边界注入攻击是唯一成功注入虚假边界的方法（PGD和AdvPatch完全失败）；两种攻击在多种对抗防御下仍有效；MIRAGE生成的图像80-84%被VLM判定为真实（AdvPatch仅0-9%）。

Conclusion: 语义级扰动作为合法的环境变化难以通过现有对抗防御缓解，暴露了当前防御方法在语义层面的根本性缺陷。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles depend on online HD map construction to perceive lane boundaries, dividers, and pedestrian crossings -- safety-critical road elements that directly govern motion planning. While existing pixel perturbation attacks can disrupt the mapping, they can be neutralized by standard adversarial defenses. We present MIRAGE, a framework for systematic discovery of semantic attacks that bypass adversarial defenses and degrade mapping predictions by finding plausible environmental variation (e.g. shadows, wet roads). MIRAGE exploits the latent manifold of real-world data learned by diffusion models, and searches for semantically mutated scenes neighboring the ground truth with the same road topology yet mislead the mapping predictions. We evaluate MIRAGE on nuScenes and demonstrate two attacks: (1) boundary removal, suppressing 57.7% of detections and corrupting 96% of planned trajectories; and (2) boundary injection, the only method that successfully injects fictitious boundaries, while pixel PGD and AdvPatch fail entirely. Both attacks remain potent under various adversarial defenses. We use two independent VLM judges to quantify realism, where MIRAGE passes as realistic 80--84% of the time (vs. 97--99% for clean nuScenes), while AdvPatch only 0--9%. Our findings expose a categorical gap in current adversarial defenses: semantic-level perturbations that manifest as legitimate environmental variation are substantially harder to mitigate than pixel-level perturbations.

</details>


### [32] [DermAgent: A Self-Reflective Agentic System for Dermatological Image Analysis with Multi-Tool Reasoning and Traceable Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14403)
*Yize Liu,Siyuan Yan,Ming Hu,Lie Ju,Xieji Li,Feilong Tang,Wei Feng,Zongyuan Ge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DermAgent是一个基于Plan-Execute-Reflect框架的多工具协作智能体，通过整合七个视觉与语言模块、双模态检索（413,210张图像+3,199条临床指南）和确定性批评家模块，在五个皮肤病学基准上显著优于GPT-4o等现有方法，诊断准确率提升17.6%，描述ROUGE-L提升3.15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大语言模型在皮肤病学诊断中缺乏领域特定的知识支撑，容易产生幻觉，无法满足临床对细粒度视觉感知和专业知识的整合需求。

Method: 提出DermAgent，核心包括三部分：1) 互补视觉感知工具（形态描述、皮镜概念标注、疾病诊断）；2) 双模态检索模块，跨参考413,210例诊断图像和3,199条临床指南块，为预测提供外部证据；3) 确定性批评家模块，通过置信度门、覆盖门和冲突门进行事后审计，自动检测来源间不一致并触发自我纠正。整体框架为Plan-Execute-Reflect。

Result: 在五个皮肤病学基准上的零样本细粒度疾病诊断、概念标注和临床描述任务中，DermAgent均优于最先进的MLLM和医学智能体基线，其中疾病诊断准确率比GPT-4o高17.6%，描述ROUGE-L高3.15%。

Conclusion: DermAgent通过多工具协作、外部知识检索和确定性审计机制，有效缓解了MLLM在皮肤病学诊断中的领域知识不足和幻觉问题，显著提升了诊断性能和可溯源性。

Abstract: Dermatological diagnosis requires integrating fine-grained visual perception with expert clinical knowledge. Although Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) facilitate interactive medical image analysis, their application in dermatology is hindered by insufficient domain-specific grounding and hallucinations. To address these issues, we propose DermAgent, a collaborative multi-tool agent that orchestrates seven specialized vision and language modules within a Plan-Execute-Reflect framework. DermAgent delivers stepwise, traceable diagnostic reasoning through three core components. First, it employs complementary visual perception tools for comprehensive morphological description, dermoscopic concept annotation, and disease diagnosis. Second, to overcome the lack of domain prior, a dual-modality retrieval module anchors every prediction in external evidence by cross-referencing 413,210 diagnosed image cases and 3,199 clinical guideline chunks. To further mitigate hallucinations, a deterministic critic module conducts strict post-hoc auditing via confidence, coverage, and conflict gates, automatically detecting inter-source disagreements to trigger targeted self-correction. Extensive experiments on five dermatology benchmarks demonstrate that DermAgent consistently outperforms state-of-the-art MLLMs and medical agent baselines across zero-shot fine-grained disease diagnosis, concept annotation, and clinical captioning tasks, exceeding GPT-4o by 17.6% in skin disease diagnostic accuracy and 3.15% in captioning ROUGE-L. Our code is available at https://github.com/YizeezLiu/DermAgent.

</details>


### [33] [ClickRemoval: An Interactive Open-Source Tool for Object Removal in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14461)
*Ledun Zhang,Yatu Ji,Xufei Zhuang,Xinying Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ClickRemoval是一个基于预训练Stable Diffusion模型、仅需用户点击即可交互式移除图像中物体的开源工具，无需额外训练、手绘遮罩或文本描述，通过自注意力调制在去噪过程中定位目标并恢复背景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物体移除工具依赖手动遮罩或文本提示，非专业用户在复杂场景中难以精确移除，常导致移除不彻底或背景补全不自然。

Method: 基于预训练的Stable Diffusion模型，无需额外训练；仅通过用户点击定位目标物体；在去噪过程中利用自注意力调制（self-attention modulation）实现目标移除与背景恢复。

Result: 在定量指标和用户研究中均取得了具有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: ClickRemoval提供了一种更简便、精确的交互式物体移除方案，并以Apache-2.0开源协议发布了完整软件包。

Abstract: Existing object removal tools often rely on manual masks or text prompts, making precise removal difficult for non-expert users in complex scenes and often leading to incomplete removal or unnatural background completion. To address this issue, we present ClickRemoval, an open-source interactive object removal tool built on pretrained Stable Diffusion models and driven solely by user clicks. Without additional training, hand-drawn masks, or text descriptions, ClickRemoval localizes target objects and restores the background through self-attention modulation during denoising. Experiments show that ClickRemoval achieves competitive results across quantitative metrics and user studies. We release a complete software package at https://github.com/zld-make/ClickRemoval under the Apache-2.0 license.

</details>


### [34] [Real2Sim in HOI: Toward Physically Plausible HOI Reconstruction from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14462)
*Yubo Zhao,Yujin Chai,Yunao Dong,Chengfeng Zhao,Zijiao Zeng,Yuan Liu,Chi-Keung Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HA-HOI是一个从单目视频中重建物理可信的4D人体-物体交互动画的框架，采用“人体优先、物体跟随”的策略，将运动轨迹投影到物理仿真中，显著提升了交互的接触一致性、时间稳定性和仿真就绪度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法能从单目视频重建人体和物体的运动轨迹，但这些轨迹在用于人形-物体仿真时存在接触不稳定、功能操作缺失和物理不合理等根本性问题，表明HOI重建不应止于追踪，而应恢复使运动成为连贯交互的关系。

Method: 提出human-first, object-follow公式：以人体运动为交互锚点，物体相对于人体动作进行重建、对齐和细化；然后将运动学轨迹投影到基于物理的人形-物体仿真中，作为教师轨迹引导稳定的物理推演。

Result: 在基准测试和真实视频上，HA-HOI在人体-物体对齐、接触一致性、时间稳定性和仿真就绪度方面均优于先前的单目HOI重建方法。

Conclusion: HA-HOI超越了视觉上合理的轨迹恢复，走向物理可信的交互动画，为将通用单目HOI视频转化为可扩展的人形-物体行为示范迈出了一步。

Abstract: Recovering 4D human-object interaction (HOI) from monocular video is a key step toward scalable 3D content creation, embodied AI, and simulation-based learning. Recent methods can reconstruct temporally coherent human and object trajectories, but these trajectories often remain visual artifacts while failing to preserve stable contact, functional manipulation, or physical plausibility when used as reference motions for humanoid-object simulation. This reveals a fundamental interaction gap: HOI reconstruction should not stop at tracking a human and an object, but should recover the relation that makes their motion a coherent interaction. We introduce $\textbf{HA-HOI}$, a framework for reconstructing physically plausible 4D HOI animation from in-the-wild monocular videos. Instead of treating the human and object as independent entities in an ambiguous monocular 3D space, we propose a $\textit{human-first, object-follow}$ formulation. The human motion is recovered as the interaction anchor, and the object is reconstructed, aligned, and refined relative to the human action. The resulting kinematic trajectory is then projected into a physics-based humanoid-object simulation, where it acts as a teacher trajectory for stable physical rollout. Across benchmark and in-the-wild videos, $\textbf{HA-HOI}$ improves human-object alignment, contact consistency, temporal stability, and simulation readiness over prior monocular HOI reconstruction methods. By moving beyond visually plausible trajectory recovery toward physically grounded interaction animation, our work takes a step toward turning general monocular HOI videos into scalable demonstrations for humanoid-object behavior. Project page: https://knoxzhao.github.io/real2sim_in_HOI/

</details>


### [35] [GeoVista: Visually Grounded Active Perception for Ultra-High-Resolution Remote Sensing Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14475)
*Jiashun Zhu,Ronghao Fu,Jiasen Hu,Nachuan Xing,Xu Na,Xiao Yang,Zhiwen Lin,Weipeng Zhang,Lang Sun,Zhiheng Xue,Haoran Liu,Weijie Zhang,Bo Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoVista提出了一种规划驱动的主动感知框架，通过全局探索计划、分支局部验证和显式证据状态管理，解决了超高分遥感图像中稀疏微小目标的多路径搜索与去重问题，在多个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感视觉语言模型使用缩放和裁剪工具进行局部区域检查，但探索策略要么是单次聚焦，要么是单一顺序轨迹。这种单路径探索会丢失全局上下文、遗漏分散区域，并导致对同一证据的重复访问或计数。

Method: 1) 提出GeoVista规划驱动主动感知框架，先构建全局探索计划，再通过分支局部验证检查多个候选区域，同时维护显式证据状态进行跨区域聚合与去重；2) 引入APEX-GRO冷启动监督轨迹语料库，将多样化的UHR任务重构为Global-Region-Object交互推理过程，采用统一尺度不变空间表示；3) 设计Observe-Plan-Track机制实现全局观察、自适应区域检查和证据追踪；4) 基于GRPO策略，使用步骤级奖励（规划、定位、最终答案正确性）对齐模型。

Result: 在RSHR-Bench、XLRS-Bench和LRS-VQA基准上取得最先进性能。

Conclusion: GeoVista通过规划驱动的多路径探索和显式证据管理，有效解决了超高分遥感图像理解中的稀疏目标搜索与去重问题，显著提升了多任务性能。

Abstract: Interpreting ultra-high-resolution (UHR) remote sensing images requires models to search for sparse and tiny visual evidence across large-scale scenes. Existing remote sensing vision-language models can inspect local regions with zooming and cropping tools, but most exploration strategies follow either a one-shot focus or a single sequential trajectory. Such single-path exploration can lose global context, leave scattered regions unvisited, and revisit or count the same evidence multiple times. To this end, we propose GeoVista, a planning-driven active perception framework for UHR remote sensing interpretation. Instead of committing to one zooming path, GeoVista first builds a global exploration plan, then verifies multiple candidate regions through branch-wise local inspection, while maintaining an explicit evidence state for cross-region aggregation and de-duplication. To enable this behavior, we introduce APEX-GRO, a cold-start supervised trajectory corpus that reformulates diverse UHR tasks as Global-Region-Object interactive reasoning processes with a unified, scale-invariant spatial representation. We further design an Observe-Plan-Track mechanism for global observation, adaptive region inspection, and evidence tracking, and align the model with a GRPO-based strategy using step-wise rewards for planning, localization, and final answer correctness. Experiments on RSHR-Bench, XLRS-Bench, and LRS-VQA show that GeoVista achieves state-of-the-art performance. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/ryan6073/GeoVista

</details>


### [36] [Head Forcing: Long Autoregressive Video Generation via Head Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14487)
*Jiahao Tian,Yiwei Wang,Gang Yu,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Head Forcing 提出了一种免训练框架，通过为自回归视频扩散模型中的不同注意力头（局部头、锚点头、记忆头）分配定制化的 KV 缓存策略，将视频生成从5秒扩展到分钟级，并支持多提示交互合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型在长序列生成中面临误差累积和上下文丢失问题，现有方法对所有注意力头采用统一处理，导致KV缓存分配次优。

Method: 发现注意力头在功能上分为三类：局部头（细节细化）、锚点头（结构稳定）和记忆头（长程上下文聚合）。为每类头设计定制化KV缓存策略：局部头和锚点头只保留关键token，记忆头采用层次化记忆系统与动态情节更新；同时引入头级RoPE重编码方案确保位置编码在预训练范围内。

Result: 无需额外训练，将生成时长从5秒扩展到分钟级，支持多提示交互合成，在各项指标上持续优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过功能导向的注意力头差异化KV缓存策略，Head Forcing有效克服了长视频生成中的误差累积和上下文丢失问题，为自回归视频扩散模型的长时生成提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models support real-time synthesis but suffer from error accumulation and context loss over long horizons. We discover that attention heads in AR video diffusion transformers serve functionally distinct roles as local heads for detail refinement, anchor heads for structural stabilization, and memory heads for long-range context aggregation, yet existing methods treat them uniformly, leading to suboptimal KV cache allocation. We propose Head Forcing, a training-free framework that assigns each head type a tailored KV cache strategy: local and anchor heads retain only essential tokens, while memory heads employ a hierarchical memory system with dynamic episodic updates for long-range consistency. A head-wise RoPE re-encoding scheme further ensures positional encodings remain within the pretrained range. Without additional training, Head Forcing extends generation from 5 seconds to minute-level duration, supports multi-prompt interactive synthesis, and consistently outperforms existing baselines. Project Page: https://jiahaotian-sjtu.github.io/headforcing.github.io/.

</details>


### [37] [HASTE: Training-Free Video Diffusion Acceleration via Head-Wise Adaptive Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14513)
*Xuzhe Zheng,Yuexiao Ma,Jing Xu,Xiawu Zheng,Rongrong Ji,Fei Chao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对视频扩散Transformer中训练无关稀疏注意力机制存在的掩码预测开销大及忽略了头级异质性导致的速度-质量权衡受限问题，提出头级自适应框架（包含时间掩码复用和误差引导的预算校准两个即插即用组件），在Wan2.1-1.3B和Wan2.1-14B上实现最高1.93倍加速且保持视频质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练无关的在线top-p稀疏注意力仍存在两个被忽视的问题：1）掩码预测带来不可忽略的计算开销；2）对各注意力头使用共享阈值，忽略了头级异质性。这两点限制了训练无关稀疏注意力在视频扩散Transformer中的实际速度-质量权衡。

Method: 提出头级自适应框架，包含两个即插即用组件：1）Temporal Mask Reuse（时间掩码复用）：基于查询-键漂移量跳过不必要的掩码预测；2）Error-guided Budgeted Calibration（误差引导的预算校准）：在全局稀疏性预算下，通过最小化模型输出误差来分配每个注意力头的top-p阈值。

Result: 在Wan2.1-1.3B和Wan2.1-14B模型上，该方法一致地改进了XAttention和SVG2，在720P分辨率下实现最高1.93倍加速，同时保持具有竞争力的视频质量和相似度指标。

Conclusion: 通过解决掩码预测开销和头级异质性两个被忽视的问题，所提出的头级自适应框架有效提升了训练无关稀疏注意力在视频扩散Transformer中的实际加速效果与质量权衡。

Abstract: Diffusion-based video generation has advanced substantially in visual fidelity and temporal coherence, but practical deployment remains limited by the quadratic complexity of full attention. Training-free sparse attention is attractive because it accelerates pretrained models without retraining, yet existing online top-$p$ sparse attention still spends non-negligible cost on mask prediction and applies shared thresholds despite strong head-level heterogeneity. We show that these two overlooked factors limit the practical speed-quality trade-off of training-free sparse attention in Video DiTs. To address them, we introduce a head-wise adaptive framework with two plug-in components: Temporal Mask Reuse, which skips unnecessary mask prediction based on query-key drift, and Error-guided Budgeted Calibration, which assigns per-head top-$p$ thresholds by minimizing measured model-output error under a global sparsity budget. On Wan2.1-1.3B and Wan2.1-14B, our method consistently improves XAttention and SVG2, achieving up to 1.93 times speedup at 720P while maintaining competitive video quality and similarity metrics.

</details>
