### [143] [AI-assisted cultural heritage dissemination: Comparing NMT and glossary-augmented LLM translation in rock art documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14679)
*Vicent Briva-Iglesias,María Ferre-Fernández*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了三种英译西设置（DeepL、Gemini-Simple、Gemini-RAG）在岩画学术文本翻译中的表现，发现通过词汇表增强提示的Gemini-RAG在术语准确率（81.4%）上显著优于其他两者，且整体翻译质量与简单提示持平，为文化遗产机构提供了低成本的术语控制方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化遗产机构在全球传播研究资料时面临多语言翻译挑战，尤其在岩画等术语密集型领域，术语准确性直接影响翻译质量和非专业读者的理解，而机构通常缺乏预算和人力进行高质量翻译。

Method: 以西班牙语岩画学术文本为源语料，设置三种英译MT方案：DeepL（强NMT基线）、Gemini-Simple（LLM+基础提示）、Gemini-RAG（LLM+词汇表增强提示+术语对检索）。采用PEARMUT框架进行人工评估，包括多维直接评估（0-100）和基于受限MQM分类的术语审计。

Result: Gemini-RAG术语精确匹配准确率最高（81.4%），Gemini-Simple为69.1%，DeepL为64.4%；整体翻译质量方面，Gemini-RAG平均DA为85.3，Gemini-Simple为85.2，均优于DeepL的80.3。

Conclusion: 词汇表增强提示是一种低开销的术语控制方法，文化遗产机构只需维护少量术语资源和轻量级评估流程即可显著提升翻译中的术语准确性。

Abstract: Cultural heritage institutions increasingly disseminate research and interpretive materials globally, but multilingual dissemination is constrained by limited budgets and staffing. In terminology-dense domains such as rock art, translation quality depends on accurate, consistent specialised terms, and small lexical errors can mislead non-specialists and reduce reuse. We compare three English MT setups for a Spanish academic rock art text, focusing on simple, operationally feasible interventions rather than complex model-side modifications: (1) DeepL as a strong NMT baseline, (2) Gemini-Simple (LLM with a basic prompt), and (3) Gemini-RAG (the same LLM with glossary-augmented prompting via term-pair retrieval). Using PEARMUT, we conduct a human evaluation via (i) multi-way Direct Assessment (0--100) and (ii) targeted terminology auditing with a restricted MQM taxonomy. Gemini-RAG yields the highest exact-match terminology accuracy (81.4\%), versus Gemini-Simple (69.1\%) and DeepL (64.4\%), while preserving overall quality (mean DA 85.3 Gemini-RAG vs. 85.2 Gemini-Simple), outperforming DeepL (80.3). These results show that glossary-augmented prompting is a low-overhead way to improve terminology control in cultural-heritage translation if institutions maintain minimal terminology resources and lightweight evaluation procedures.

</details>


### [144] [Non-linear Interventions on Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14749)
*Sangwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通用的干预框架，将干预扩展到非线性流形上编码的特征，并支持对无直接输出签名的隐式特征进行干预，在拒绝绕过引导任务上比线性基线更精确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有干预方法局限于线性干预，无法处理沿非线性流形编码的特征，需要一种能处理非线性表示特征的通用干预方法。

Method: 提出一种通用的干预公式，自然扩展到非线性表示的特征；同时设计一种学习过程，使干预能够作用于缺乏直接输出签名的隐式特征。

Result: 在拒绝绕过引导任务上，该方法通过干预控制拒绝行为的非线性特征，比线性基线方法实现了更精确的模型引导。

Conclusion: 该通用干预框架突破了线性干预的局限，能有效处理非线性特征和隐式特征，为理解和控制LLM内部表征提供了更强大的工具。

Abstract: Intervention is one of the most representative and widely used methods for understanding the internal representations of large language models (LLMs). However, existing intervention methods are confined to linear interventions grounded in the Linear Representation Hypothesis, leaving features encoded along non-linear manifolds beyond their reach. In this work, we introduce a general formulation of intervention that extends naturally to non-linearly represented features, together with a learning procedure that further enables intervention on implicit features lacking a direct output signature. We validate our framework on refusal bypass steering, where it steers the model more precisely than linear baselines by intervening on a non-linear feature governing refusal.

</details>


### [145] [Streaming Speech-to-Text Translation with a SpeechLLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14766)
*Titouan Parcollet,Shucong Zhang,Xianrui Zheng,Rogier C. van Dalen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于LLM的实时流式语音到文本翻译架构，通过学习自主决定何时已听到足够音频来输出翻译，在1-2秒延迟下达到接近非流式基线的翻译质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SpeechLLM系统无法实现真正的实时流式翻译，要么等待完整音频结束才输出，要么以固定间隔输出，不适合实际应用。

Method: 采用LLM架构，模型不仅学习输出翻译token，还学习自主判断是否已接收足够音频来输出；使用输入语音与输出文本的自动对齐进行训练。

Result: 在不同语言对上，系统在仅1-2秒延迟下达到接近非流式基线的翻译质量。

Conclusion: 提出的方法能够实现真正的实时流式语音翻译，显著降低延迟的同时保持翻译质量。

Abstract: Normally, a system that translates speech into text consists of separate modules for speech recognition and text-to-text translation. Combining those tasks into a SpeechLLM promises to exploit paralinguistic information in the speech and to reduce cascaded errors. But existing SpeechLLM systems are slow since they do not work in a real streaming fashion: they wait for a complete utterance of audio before outputting a translation, or output tokens at fixed intervals, which is not suitable for real applications. This work proposes an LLM-based architecture for real streaming speech-to-text translation. The LLM learns not just to emit output tokens, but also to decide whether it has seen enough audio to do so. The system is trained using automatic alignments of the input speech and the output text. In experiments on different language pairs, the system achieves a translation quality close to the non-streaming baseline, but with a latency of only 1-2 seconds.

</details>


### [146] [Graphs of Research: Citation Evolution Graphs as Supervision for Research Idea Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14790)
*Songyang Gao,Yinghui Xia,Siyi Liu,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Graphs of Research (GoR)方法，通过构建论文演化有向无环图，将引文结构关系作为监督信号微调大语言模型，实现科学想法的自动生成，在多轮LLM裁判对比中达到SOTA效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的想法生成方法主要依赖静态文献检索或复杂提示工程，未能充分利用参考文献间的结构关系，导致生成质量受限。

Method: 构建一个自动化提取pipeline，为每篇种子论文提取2跳引用邻域，从引用位置、频次、前驱链接和发表时间推导演进关系，组织成论文演化有向无环图(DAG)；基于Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-1M，在包含引文图、边信号、参考文献信息和任务定义的结构化文本提示上进行监督微调。

Result: 在GPT-4o驱动的头对头LLM裁判锦标赛中，GoR-SFT击败了多个基线方法，达到当前最优性能。

Conclusion: 引文演化图作为监督信号能有效提升LLM科学想法自动生成的质量，有望降低该方向的使用门槛，加速自动化科学创新。

Abstract: Research idea generation is the innovation-driving step of automated scientific research. Recently, large language models (LLMs) have shown potential for automating idea generation at scale. However, existing methods mainly condition LLMs on eliciting idea generation through static retrieval of relevant literature or complex prompt engineering, without discarding the structural relations among references. We propose Graphs of Research (GoR), a supervised fine-tuning method that extracts a 2-hop reference neighborhood for each seed paper, derives the relations among those references from citation position, frequency, predecessor links, and publication time, and organizes them into a paper-evolution directed acyclic graph (DAG). We construct an automated extraction pipeline that draws data from five major ML/NLP venues, comprising 498/50/50 train/validation/test seed papers and approximately 7,600 cited references. Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-1M is fine-tuned on a structured-text prompt that includes the citation graph, edge signals, reference information, and task definition to predict the idea for the seed paper. Across head-to-head LLM-judge tournaments against gpt-4o-driven baselines, GoR-SFT achieves SOTA, demonstrating the effectiveness of citation-evolution graphs as supervision signal for LLM-based idea generation. We hope that this reduces the barrier for citation evolution graphs as a supervision, accelerating automated scientific innovation.

</details>


### [147] [Conversion of Lexicon-Grammar tables to LMF. Application to French](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14816)
*Eric Laporte,Elsa Tolone,Mathieu Constant*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 首次将法语动词的词典语法表转换为LMF格式的实验，实现了法语词汇句法资源的标准化和互操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法语词典语法是法语词汇和句法信息的主要来源之一，但缺乏互操作表示格式，限制了其在不同自然语言处理场景中的使用。

Method: 将法语动词的词典语法表转换为LMF（词汇标记框架）格式，分析转换过程中的主要难点，并描述最终生成的资源。

Result: 成功将词典语法表转换为LMF格式，生成了具有互操作性的词汇资源。

Conclusion: 该转换使得法语词典语法资源符合LMF标准，提升了自然语言处理词典的标准化和互操作性，使其可在不同上下文中使用。

Abstract: We describe the first experiment of conversion of Lexicon-Grammar tables for French verbs into the Lexical Markup Framework (LMF) format. The Lexicon-Grammar of the French language is currently one of the major sources of lexical and syntactic information for French. Its conversion into an interoperable representation format according to the LMF standard makes it usable in different contexts, thus contributing to the standardization and interoperability of natural language processing dictionaries. We briefly introduce the Lexicon-Grammar and the derived dictionaries; we analyse the main difficulties faced during the conversion; and we describe the resulting resource.

</details>


### [148] [Tokenizer Fertility and Zero-Shot Performance of Foundation Models on Ukrainian Legal Text: A Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14890)
*Volodymyr Ovcharov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 对乌克兰法律文本的7种基础模型进行基准测试，发现tokenizer效率差异达1.6倍，零样本优于少样本，NVIDIA Nemotron Super 3以更低成本取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乌克兰法律领域缺乏对不同基础模型在tokenizer效率和任务性能方面的系统性对比研究。

Method: 从EDRSR选取273份经核实的法院判决，对5家供应商的7种模型进行tokenizer fertility测量及三项任务的零样本性能基准测试。

Result: (1) Tokenizer fertility差异达1.6倍，Qwen3比Llama系列多消耗60% tokens；(2) NVIDIA Nemotron Super 3 (120B)综合得分83.1，以1/3 API成本超越Mistral Large 3；(3) 少样本提示导致性能下降高达26个百分点，消融实验证实该问题源于乌克兰语演示的内在特性。

Conclusion: 从业者应在模型选择前进行tokenizer分析；对于形态丰富的语言，零样本比少样本更为可靠。

Abstract: Foundation models tokenize Ukrainian legal text with vastly different efficiency, yet no systematic comparison exists for this domain. We benchmark seven models from five providers on 273 validated court decisions from Ukraine's state registry (EDRSR), measuring tokenizer fertility and zero-shot performance on three tasks. Three findings emerge. (1) Tokenizer fertility varies 1.6x: Qwen3 models consume 60% more tokens than Llama-family models on identical input, directly reducing API cost. (2) NVIDIA Nemotron Super 3 (120B) achieves the highest composite score (83.1), outperforming Mistral Large 3 (675B total, 41B active) -- a model with 5.6x more total parameters and 3.4x more active parameters per token -- at one-third the API cost. (3) Few-shot prompting degrades performance by up to 26 percentage points; stratified and prompt-sensitivity ablations confirm this is intrinsic to Ukrainian-language demonstrations, not an artifact of example selection. For practitioners: tokenizer analysis should precede model selection, and zero-shot is a more reliable default than few-shot for morphologically rich languages.

</details>


### [149] [Chain-of-Procedure: Hierarchical Visual-Language Reasoning for Procedural QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14928)
*Guanhua Chen,Yutong Yao,Shenghe Sun,Ci-Jun Gao,Shudong Liu,Lidia S. Chao,Feng Wan,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ProcedureVQA基准测试和Chain-of-Procedure (CoP)框架，用于评估和提升视觉语言模型在视觉程序问答（VP-QA）任务上的表现，CoP通过分层推理实现了高达13%的绝对性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在标准图文任务上表现优异，但在视觉程序问答（VP-QA）这一实际任务上的潜力尚未被充分探索，现有模型存在跨模态检索不足和图像序列粒度与文本步骤分解不匹配的问题。

Method: 提出Chain-of-Procedure (CoP)分层推理框架，包含三个步骤：首先利用视觉线索检索相关指令，然后通过语义分解进行步骤精炼，最后生成下一步操作。

Result: 在六个视觉语言模型上的实验表明，CoP框架相比标准基线实现了高达13%的绝对性能提升。

Conclusion: ProcedureVQA有效评估了VLMs在视觉程序推理上的能力，CoP框架通过分层推理显著缓解了跨模态检索和粒度对齐问题，为VP-QA任务提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved impressive results on standard image-text tasks, yet their potential for visual procedure question answering (VP-QA) remains largely unexplored. VP-QA presents unique challenges where users query next-step actions by uploading images for intermediate states of complex procedures. To systematically evaluate VLMs on this practical task, we propose ProcedureVQA, a novel multimodal benchmark specifically designed for visual procedural reasoning. Through comprehensive analysis, we identify two critical limitations in current VLMs: inadequate cross-modal retrieval of structured procedures given visual states, and misalignment between image sequence granularity and textual step decomposition. To address these issues, we present Chain-of-Procedure (CoP), a hierarchical reasoning framework that first retrieves relevant instructions using visual cues, then performs step refinement through semantic decomposition, and finally generates the next step. Experiments across six VLMs demonstrate CoP's effectiveness, achieving up to 13% absolute improvement over standard baselines.

</details>


### [150] [Performance-Driven Policy Optimization for Speculative Decoding with Adaptive Windowing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14978)
*Jie Jiang,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PPOW框架，通过窗口级强化学习优化投机解码中的草稿模型，替代传统token级监督学习，实现6.29-6.52平均接受长度和3.39-4.36倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投机解码中，草稿模型使用token级监督目标优化，但投机效率本质上是窗口级且对前缀敏感的，早期不匹配会截断已接受前缀并使后续推测窗口失效，导致效率瓶颈。

Method: 提出PPOW框架，包含三个核心组件：Cost-Aware Speedup Reward（代价感知加速奖励）、Distribution-Based Proximity Reward（基于分布的邻近奖励）和Adaptive Divergence-Aware Windowing（自适应分歧感知窗口），将草稿模型优化从token级模仿转向窗口级强化学习优化。

Result: 在多个模型族和基准测试中，统一解码协议下平均接受长度为6.29-6.52，加速比达到3.39-4.36倍。

Conclusion: 性能驱动的窗口级优化是提升投机解码效率的实用方法。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by having a lightweight draft model propose speculative windows of candidate tokens for parallel verification by a larger target model. In practice, speculative efficiency is often bottlenecked by hard-to-draft positions, where an early mismatch truncates the accepted prefix and invalidates the rest of the speculative window. Most learning-based drafters are still optimized with token-level supervised objectives, even though speculative utility is inherently window-level and prefix-sensitive. We propose PPOW (Performance-Driven Policy Optimization with Adaptive Windowing), a reinforcement learning framework that shifts drafter optimization from token-level imitation to window-level optimization. PPOW combines a Cost-Aware Speedup Reward, a Distribution-Based Proximity Reward, and Adaptive Divergence-Aware Windowing, which prioritizes informative windows with high confidence-weighted draft-target divergence. PPOW achieves average acceptance lengths of 6.29-6.52 and speedups of 3.39-4.36$\times$ across multiple model families and benchmarks under a unified decoding protocol. These results show that performance-driven window-level optimization is a practical approach to improving speculative decoding efficiency.

</details>


### [151] [Quantifying and Mitigating Premature Closure in Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15000)
*Rebecca Handler,Suhana Bedi,Nigam Shah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLMs）在医学任务中的“过早闭合”现象，即在不确定情况下不当给出答案而非选择澄清或拒绝。评估了五个前沿模型，发现在MedQA、AfriMed-QA、HealthBench和对抗性查询中，模型存在高比例的虚假行动率（53-82%），安全提示可降低但无法消除该问题，表明需要评估LLMs“何时不应回答”的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 诊断错误中“过早闭合”（在信息不足时过早下结论）是一个已知因素，但在LLMs中未被充分研究。作者旨在定义和量化LLM的过早闭合现象，即模型在不确定时不当提供答案而非选择更安全的回应（如澄清、放弃、升级或拒绝）。

Method: 定义了LLM过早闭合的概念，并在两类任务中评估五个前沿LLM：（1）结构化医学问答（MedQA 500题、AfriMed-QA 490题），移除正确选项后观察模型是否仍选答案；（2）开放式医学任务（HealthBench 861题、191个由医生撰写的对抗性查询），评估模型给出不当答案的比例。同时测试了安全导向提示的缓解效果。

Result: 在MedQA和AfriMed-QA中，模型在正确选项被移除后仍高频率选择答案，基线虚假行动率分别为55-81%和53-82%。在开放式评估中，模型在HealthBench平均30%的问题上给出不当答案，在对抗性查询中高达78%。安全提示可降低过早闭合率，但残留故障仍然存在。

Conclusion: LLM在医学任务中存在显著的过早闭合问题，即在不确定时仍给出答案而非选择更安全的回应。安全导向提示有一定缓解作用但无法完全消除。研究强调了评估医学LLM“何时不应回答”能力的重要性。

Abstract: Premature closure, or committing to a conclusion before sufficient information is available, is a recognized contributor to diagnostic error but remains underexamined in large language models (LLMs). We define LLM premature closure as inappropriate commitment under uncertainty: providing an answer, recommendation, or clinical guidance when the safer response would be clarification, abstention, escalation, or refusal. We evaluated five frontier LLMs across structured and open-ended medical tasks. In MedQA (n = 500) and AfriMed-QA (n = 490) questions where the correct choice had been removed, models still selected an answer at high rates, with baseline false-action rates of 55-81% and 53-82%, respectively. In open-ended evaluation, models gave inappropriate answers on an average of 30% of 861 HealthBench questions and 78% of 191 physician-authored adversarial queries. Safety-oriented prompting reduced premature closure across models, but residual failure persisted, highlighting the need to evaluate whether medical LLMs know when not to answer.

</details>


### [152] [The Scientific Contribution Graph: Automated Literature-based Technological Roadmapping at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15011)
*Peter A. Jansen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了自动化技术路线图绘制任务，通过从学术论文中提取科学贡献并链接其前提条件，构建了包含200万条详细科学贡献和1250万条前提关系的大规模AI/NLP领域资源——科学贡献图，并引入科学前提预测任务，实验表明当代模型在该任务上表现快速提升（时间过滤回测中MAP达0.48）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学贡献很少孤立发展，而是建立在先前发现之上。目前缺乏自动化方法从学术文献中提取科学贡献并链接其前提关系，以支持技术路线图绘制、科学影响评估和自动化科学发现。

Method: 1. 构建科学贡献图（Scientific Contribution Graph）：从23万篇开放获取论文中提取200万条详细科学贡献，并通过1250万条前提边进行连接；2. 引入科学前提预测任务：预测哪些现有技术可以促成未来发现；3. 使用时间过滤回测（temporally filtered backtesting）评估模型性能。

Result: 构建了AI/NLP领域的大规模资源——科学贡献图，包含200万科学贡献和1250万前提关系；在科学前提预测任务上，当代模型表现快速提升，时间过滤回测中平均精度均值（MAP）达到0.48。

Conclusion: 自动化技术路线图绘制资源（如科学贡献图）将支持科学影响评估和自动化科学发现，且科学前提预测任务有望推动相关模型进一步发展。

Abstract: Scientific contributions rarely develop in isolation, but instead build upon prior discoveries. We formulate the task of automated technological roadmapping as extracting scientific contributions from scholarly articles and linking them to their prerequisites. We present the Scientific Contribution Graph, a large-scale AI/NLP-domain resource containing 2 million detailed scientific contributions extracted from 230k open-access papers and connected by 12.5 million prerequisite edges. We further introduce scientific prerequisite prediction, a scientific discovery task in which models predict which existing technologies can enable future discoveries, and show that contemporary models are rapidly improving on this task, reaching 0.48 MAP when evaluated using temporally filtered backtesting. We anticipate technological roadmapping resources such as this will support scientific impact assessment and automated scientific discovery.

</details>


### [153] [COTCAgent: Preventive Consultation via Probabilistic Chain-of-Thought Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15016)
*Zihan Deng,Xiaozhen Zhong,Chuanzhi Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为COTCAgent的分层推理框架，用于解决大语言模型在纵向电子健康记录推理中的统计幻觉和时序依赖性捕获困难两大问题。该框架包含时序统计适配器(TSA)、链式思维补全(COTC)层和有界补全模块，通过解耦统计计算、特征匹配和语言生成，在自建数据集和HealthBench上分别达到90.47%和70.41%的Top-1准确率，优于现有医疗智能体和主流大语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在纵向电子健康记录推理中存在两大缺陷：一是缺乏细粒度统计推理能力，在文本隐含定量证据时会产生临床趋势和指标的幻觉，导致诊断偏差；二是纵向EHR中的非均匀时间序列和稀缺标签阻碍模型捕获长程时间依赖，限制可靠临床推理。

Method: 提出了COTCAgent，一个分层推理框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 时序统计适配器(TSA)，将分析计划转化为可执行代码用于标准化趋势输出；(2) 链式思维补全(COTC)层，利用症状-趋势-疾病知识库结合加权评分评估疾病风险；(3) 有界补全模块，通过标准化查询和迭代评分约束获取结构化证据。框架通过解耦统计计算、特征匹配和语言生成，避免复杂多模态输入。

Result: 基于Baichuan-M2驱动的COTCAgent在自建数据集上达到90.47%的Top-1准确率，在HealthBench上达到70.41%，优于现有医疗智能体和主流大语言模型。

Conclusion: 通过将统计计算、特征匹配和语言生成解耦，该框架有效解决了大语言模型在纵向EHR推理中的统计幻觉和时序依赖问题，能够以较低计算开销实现高效的纵向记录分析。

Abstract: As large language models empower healthcare, intelligent clinical decision support has developed rapidly. Longitudinal electronic health records (EHR) provide essential temporal evidence for accurate clinical diagnosis and analysis. However, current large language models have critical flaws in longitudinal EHR reasoning. First, lacking fine-grained statistical reasoning, they often hallucinate clinical trends and metrics when quantitative evidence is textually implied, biasing diagnostic inference. Second, non-uniform time series and scarce labels in longitudinal EHR hinder models from capturing long-range temporal dependencies, limiting reliable clinical reasoning. To address the above limitations, this work presents the Probabilistic Chain-of-Thought Completion Agent (COTCAgent), a hierarchical reasoning framework for longitudinal electronic health records. It consists of three core modules. The Temporal-Statistics Adapter (TSA) converts analytical plans into executable code for standardized trend output. The Chain-of-Thought Completion (COTC) layer leverages a symptom-trend-disease knowledge base with weighted scoring to evaluate disease risk, while the bounded completion module acquires structured evidence through standardized inquiries and iterative scoring constraints to ensure rigorous reasoning. By decoupling statistical computation, feature matching, and language generation, the framework eliminates reliance on complex multi-modal inputs and enables efficient longitudinal record analysis with lower computational overhead. Experimental results show that COTCAgent powered by Baichuan-M2 achieves 90.47% Top-1 accuracy on the self-built dataset and 70.41% on HealthBench, outperforming existing medical agents and mainstream large language models. The code is available at https://github.com/FrankDengAI/COTCAgent/.

</details>


### [154] [From Scenes to Elements: Multi-Granularity Evidence Retrieval for Verifiable Multimodal RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15019)
*Guanhua Chen,Chuyue Huang,Yutong Yao,Shudong Liu,Xueqing Song,Lidia S. Chao,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GranuVistaVQA基准和GranuRAG框架，通过将检索粒度从整个图像/场景细化为视觉元素级别，解决多模态RAG系统中粗粒度检索与细粒度用户查询不匹配的问题，实验显示相比六个强基线方法性能提升高达29.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态RAG系统以整个图像或场景为检索单元，粒度太粗，无法匹配细粒度的用户查询，且失败模式难以验证。为解决这一粒度不匹配问题，需要建立元素级别的检索和生成机制。

Method: 1) 构建GranuVistaVQA基准，包含真实地标的多视角元素级标注，体现部分观察挑战；2) 提出GranuRAG多粒度框架，包含三个阶段：元素级检测与分类、多粒度跨模态对齐检索、属性约束生成，将视觉元素作为一等检索单元。

Result: 在GranuVistaVQA基准上，GranuRAG相比六个强基线方法实现了最高29.2%的性能提升。

Conclusion: 将检索粒度下放到元素级别，取代隐式注意力机制，能够实现透明的错误诊断，显著提升多模态RAG在细粒度查询任务上的表现。

Abstract: Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems retrieve evidence at coarse granularities (entire images or scenes), creating a mismatch with fine-grained user queries and making failures unverifiable. We introduce GranuVistaVQA, a multimodal benchmark featuring real-world landmarks with element-level annotations across multiple viewpoints, capturing the partial observation challenge where individual images contain only subsets of entities. We further propose GranuRAG, a multi-granularity framework that treats visual elements as first-class retrieval units through three stages: element-level detection and classification, multi-granularity cross-modal alignment for evidence retrieval, and attribution-constrained generation. By grounding retrieval at the element level rather than relying on implicit attention, our approach enables transparent error diagnosis. Experiments demonstrate that GranuRAG achieves up to 29.2% improvement over six strong baselines for this task.

</details>


### [155] [AI Knows When It's Being Watched: Functional Strategic Action and Contextual Register Modulation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15034)
*Vinicius Covas,Jorge Alberto Hidalgo Toledo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过100场多智能体辩论实验，发现基于LLM的多智能体系统在感知到社会观察时会表现出系统的语言适应——词汇多样性提升（监控条件下TTR变化+24.9%），且对人类观察者比对AI观察者表现出更强的语域形式化（Delta+24.9% vs +22.2%），而观众框架条件效果较弱（+17.7%）；消息长度则呈现完全分离的效应模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在计算和认知层面已被广泛研究，但其作为社会结构化语境中的交流主体的行为尚不明确。本研究旨在考察LLM多智能体系统是否会在感知到社会观察时表现出系统的语言适应，这一发现对AI治理和审计具有直接启示。

Method: 基于哈贝马斯沟通行动理论、戈夫曼拟剧论、贝尔受众设计框架以及霍桑效应，设计控制实验：100场多智能体辩论会话，分为5种条件（每组n=20），操纵社会观察框架（明确监控、否定监控、自动AI审计系统替代人类观察者等）。测量指标：TTR（类符-形符比）和消息长度。

Result: 监控条件（Delta+24.9%, +24.2%）和自动AI监控条件（Delta+22.2%）的TTR变化显著高于观众框架条件（Delta+17.7%），F(4,94)=2.79, p=.031。消息长度呈现完全分离的效应，F(4,95)=19.55, p<.001。用AI观察者替代人类观察者的条件产生中等TTR适应，表明LLM对观察者身份敏感。

Conclusion: LLM行为对感知到的社会观察框架敏感，人类评估比自动AI监控更能引发语域形式化。这启示我们需要将LLM重新定位为具有语境敏感性的沟通行动者，对AI治理和算法审计具有重要含义。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been extensively studied from computational and cognitive perspectives, yet their behavior as communicative actors in socially structured contexts remains underexplored. This study examines whether LLM-based multi-agent systems exhibit systematic linguistic adaptation in response to perceived social observation contexts -- a question with direct implications for AI governance and auditing. Drawing on Habermas's (1981) Theory of Communicative Action, Goffman's (1959) dramaturgical model, Bell's (1984) Audience Design framework, and the Hawthorne Effect, we report a controlled experiment involving 100 multi-agent debate sessions across five conditions (n = 20 each). Conditions varied the framing of social observation -- from explicit monitoring by university researchers, to negation of monitoring, to an observer-substitution condition replacing human researchers with an automated AI auditing system. Monitored conditions (Delta+24.9%, Delta+24.2%) and the automated AI monitoring condition (Delta+22.2%) produce higher TTR change than audience-framing conditions (Delta+17.7%), F(4, 94) = 2.79, p = .031. Message length shows a fully dissociated effect, F(4, 95) = 19.55, p < .001. A fifth condition -- replacing human with AI observers -- yields intermediate TTR adaptation, suggesting LLM behavior is sensitive to observer identity: human evaluation elicits stronger register formalization than automated AI surveillance. We discuss implications for AI governance, algorithmic auditing, and the repositioning of LLMs as contextually sensitive communicative actors.

</details>


### [156] [ML-Embed: Inclusive and Efficient Embeddings for a Multilingual World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15081)
*Ziyin Zhang,Zihan Liao,Hang Yu,Peng Di,Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ML-Embed 提出3D-ML框架（MRL+MLL+MEL），通过多语言数据集和全生命周期效率优化，在430个任务中取得SOTA，尤其擅长低资源语言，并开源所有模型、数据和代码，打破了计算成本、语言覆盖和透明度三大壁垒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本嵌入模型面临三个关键障碍：高昂的计算成本、语言覆盖狭窄（忽略大多数语言）、以及闭源或开放权重模型缺乏透明度阻碍研究。作者旨在拆除这些壁垒，构建公平且高效的多语言嵌入系统。

Method: 提出3D-ML框架，包含三个维度：MRL（存储效率）、MLL（推理深度灵活选择）、以及新引入的MEL（参数效率）。同时构建大规模多语言数据集，训练140M到8B参数的模型系列，并承诺完全开源。

Result: 在430个任务上进行评估，在17个MTEB基准中的9个上刷新记录，尤其在低资源语言上表现突出。

Conclusion: ML-Embed提供了可复现的蓝图，展示了如何构建全球公平且计算高效的AI系统，打破了现有嵌入模型在成本、语言覆盖和透明度方面的壁垒。

Abstract: The development of high-quality text embeddings is increasingly drifting toward an exclusionary future, defined by three critical barriers: prohibitive computational costs, a narrow linguistic focus that neglects most of the world's languages, and a lack of transparency from closed-source or open-weight models that stifles research. To dismantle these barriers, we introduce ML-Embed, a suite of inclusive and efficient models built upon a new framework: 3-Dimensional Matryoshka Learning (3D-ML). Our framework addresses the computational challenge with comprehensive efficiency across the entire model lifecycle. Beyond the storage benefits of Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL) and flexible inference-time depth provided by Matryoshka Layer Learning (MLL), we introduce Matryoshka Embedding Learning (MEL) for enhanced parameter efficiency. To address the linguistic challenge, we curate a massively multilingual dataset and train a suite of models ranging from 140M to 8B parameters. In a direct commitment to transparency, we release all models, data, and code. Extensive evaluation on 430 tasks demonstrates that our models set new records on 9 of 17 evaluated MTEB benchmarks, with particularly strong results in low-resource languages, providing a reproducible blueprint for building globally equitable and computationally efficient AI systems.

</details>


### [157] [Improving Multi-turn Dialogue Consistency with Self-Recall Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15102)
*Renning Pang,Tian Lan,Leyuan Liu,Xiaoming Huang,Piao Tong,Xiaosong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Self-Recall Thinking (SRT)框架，通过自回忆链机制让LLM在多轮对话中选择性召回历史有用轮次，在提升长程依赖跟踪能力的同时降低推理延迟，在多个数据集上F1提升4.7%、延迟降低14.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮对话系统中LLM难以跟踪非相邻轮次间的依赖关系，随着对话增长，关键信息稀疏且被无关上下文淹没，处理完整历史导致严重效率瓶颈；现有方案要么依赖高延迟外部记忆，要么通过迭代摘要丢失细粒度细节。

Method: SRT框架包含三个核心组件：(1) Dependency Construction：构建历史轮次间的依赖关系并转化为自回忆链；(2) Capability Initialization：训练模型具备回忆令牌推理链的能力；(3) Reasoning Improvement：通过可验证奖励优化召回和推理准确性。

Result: 在多个数据集上，SRT相比现有方法F1分数提升4.7%，端到端延迟降低14.7%，在推理延迟与准确性之间取得良好平衡，超越当前最先进基线。

Conclusion: SRT通过内生推理过程实现可解释的回忆步骤，无需外部模块即可选择性召回历史上下文，有效解决了长程依赖和稀疏信息问题，在准确性和效率上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) based multi-turn dialogue systems often struggle to track dependencies across non-adjacent turns, undermining both consistency and scalability. As conversations lengthen, essential information becomes sparse and is buried in irrelevant context, while processing the entire dialogue history incurs severe efficiency bottlenecks. Existing solutions either rely on high latency external memory or lose fine-grained details through iterative summarization. In this paper, we propose Self-Recall Thinking (SRT), a framework designed to address long-range contextual dependency and sparse informative signals in multi-turn dialogue. SRT identifies helpful historical turns and uses them to generate contextually appropriate responses, enabling the model to selectively recall and reason over context during inference. This process yields an endogenous reasoning process that integrates interpretable recall steps without external modules. SRT incorporates: (1) Dependency Construction: Generating and converting it into self-recall chains; (2)Capability Initialization: Training to enable reasoning chains with recall tokens capability; (3)Reasoning Improvement: Refining accuracy via verifiable rewards to optimize recall and reasoning for correct answers. Experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that SRT improves F1 score by 4.7% and reduces end-to-end latency by 14.7% over prior methods, achieving a balance between reasoning latency and accuracy, and outperforming state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [158] [From Text to Voice: A Reproducible and Verifiable Framework for Evaluating Tool Calling LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15104)
*Md Tahmid Rahman Laskar,Xue-Yong Fu,Seyyed Saeed Sarfjoo,Quinten McNamara,Jonas Robertson,Shashi Bhushan TN*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种数据集无关的框架，将已有的文本工具调用基准通过TTS、说话人变化和环境噪声转换为音频评估，无需重新标注。在7个全模态模型上的评估显示性能高度依赖于模型和任务，文本到语音的性能差距在1.8-4.8分之间，主要错误源于对参数值的语音理解错误。还验证了开源Qwen3法官（≥8B）与专有法官的一致性超过80%，支持隐私保护评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音智能体需要可靠的工具调用能力，但现有的工具调用基准都是基于文本的，缺乏可控的音频工具调用评估方法。需要一种无需重新标注工具模式和标签、可验证且可复现的音频评估框架。

Method: 提出数据集无关的框架，使用文本转语音、说话人变化和环境噪声将文本基准转换为配对文本-音频实例，保留原始标注。在Confetti和When2Call的音频转换版本上评估7个全模态模型，并设计歧义重述压力测试、LLM-as-judge无参考协议等。

Result: Gemini-3.1-Flash-Live在Confetti上得分最高（70.4），GPT-Realtime-1.5在When2Call上最佳（71.9）。Confetti上文本到语音差距从Qwen3-Omni的1.8分到GPT-Realtime-1.5的4.8分。开源Qwen3法官（≥8B）与专有法官一致性超过80%。

Conclusion: 提出的框架提供了一种可验证、可复现的一阶段诊断方法，补充了专门构建的音频语料库；性能高度依赖于模型和任务；语音理解中的参数值误解是主要错误来源；开源模型可用于隐私保护评估。

Abstract: Voice agents increasingly require reliable tool use from speech, whereas prominent tool-calling benchmarks remain text-based. We study whether verified text benchmarks can be converted into controlled audio-based tool calling evaluations without re-annotating the tool schema and gold labels. Our dataset-agnostic framework uses text-to-speech, speaker variation, and environmental noise to create paired text-audio instances while preserving the original dataset annotations. Based on extensive evaluation of 7 omni-modal models on audio-converted versions of Confetti and When2Call, our framework demonstrates that the performance is strongly model- and task-dependent: Gemini-3.1-Flash-Live obtains the highest Confetti score (70.4), whereas GPT-Realtime-1.5 performs best on When2Call (71.9). On Confetti, the text-to-voice gap ranges from 1.8 points for Qwen3-Omni to 4.8 points for GPT-Realtime-1.5. A targeted analysis of failure cases demonstrates that degradations most often reflect misunderstandings of argument values in the speech. Considering real-world deployment scenarios, we further report text-only results, an ambiguity-based reformulation stress test, and a reference-free LLM-as-judge protocol validated against human preferences. Notably, we find that open-source Qwen3 judges with at least 8B parameters exceed 80% agreement with proprietary judges, supporting privacy-preserving evaluation. Overall, our framework provides a verifiable and reproducible first-stage diagnostic that complements purpose-built audio corpora.

</details>


### [159] [MeMo: Memory as a Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15156)
*Ryan Wei Heng Quek,Sanghyuk Lee,Alfred Wei Lun Leong,Arun Verma,Alok Prakash,Nancy F. Chen,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low,Daniela Rus,Armando Solar-Lezama*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MeMo是一种模块化框架，通过专用记忆模型编码新知识，在不修改LLM参数的情况下实现知识注入，无需访问模型权重，检索成本与语料库规模无关，在三个基准测试上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在预训练后保持冻结状态，无法高效获取实时、特定领域的新知识，现有方法存在检索噪声敏感、灾难性遗忘、依赖模型权重访问等问题，亟需一种高效、鲁棒且与模型无关的知识注入机制。

Method: 提出MeMo（Memory as a Model）模块化框架，将新知识编码到独立的记忆模型中，保持LLM参数不变。该框架能捕捉跨文档关系、对检索噪声鲁棒、避免灾难性遗忘、无需访问LLM权重或logits（支持开源和闭源LLM即插即用），且推理时检索成本与语料库规模无关。

Result: 在BrowseComp-Plus、NarrativeQA和MuSiQue三个基准测试上，MeMo相比现有方法在多种设置下均取得强性能。

Conclusion: MeMo提供了一种高效、灵活且与模型无关的知识注入方案，在保持LLM原有能力的同时有效融合新知识，具有良好的实用性和扩展性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance across a wide range of tasks, but remain frozen after pretraining until subsequent updates. Many real-world applications require timely, domain-specific information, motivating the need for efficient mechanisms to incorporate new knowledge. In this paper, we introduce MeMo (Memory as a Model), a modular framework that encodes new knowledge into a dedicated memory model while keeping the LLM parameters unchanged. Compared to existing methods, MeMo offers several advantages: (a) it captures complex cross-document relationships, (b) it is robust to retrieval noise, (c) it avoids catastrophic forgetting in the LLM, (d) it does not require access to the LLM's weights or output logits, enabling plug-and-play integration with both open and proprietary closed-source LLMs, and (e) its retrieval cost is independent of corpus size at inference time. Our experimental results on three benchmarks, BrowseComp-Plus, NarrativeQA, and MuSiQue, show that MeMo achieves strong performance compared to existing methods across diverse settings.

</details>


### [160] [Text Knows What, Tables Know When: Clinical Timeline Reconstruction via Retrieval-Augmented Multimodal Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15168)
*Sayantan Kumar,Shahriar Noroozizadeh,Juyong Kim,Jeremy C. Weiss*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种检索增强的多模态对齐框架，通过将非结构化临床文本与结构化EHR数据进行对齐，以提升从文本中提取的绝对临床时间线的时间精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化临床叙事语义丰富但缺乏时间精度，结构化EHR数据时间精确但缺失大量临床事件。现有方法无法兼顾两者优势，导致临床时间线重建不完整或不精确。

Method: 将时间线重建建模为基于图的多步骤过程：1) 从叙事中提取中心锚定事件构建初始时间骨架；2) 将非中心事件相对于该骨架进行定位；3) 利用检索的结构化EHR行作为外部时间证据对时间线进行校准。使用指令微调的大语言模型在i2m4基准（MIMIC-III/IV）上评估。

Result: 多模态管道在几乎所有评估模型上一致提升了绝对时间戳准确性（AULTC）和时间一致性，且未牺牲事件匹配率。34.8%的文本事件在表格记录中完全缺失，表明多模态对齐能产生更忠实、更具临床信息量的重建。

Conclusion: 对齐文本与结构化EHR数据可以构建比单一数据源更时间精确且临床信息更丰富的患者轨迹重建。

Abstract: Reconstructing precise clinical timelines is essential for modeling patient trajectories and forecasting risk in complex, heterogeneous conditions like sepsis. While unstructured clinical narratives offer semantically rich and contextually complete descriptions of a patient's course, they often lack temporal precision and contain ambiguous event timing. Conversely, structured electronic health record (EHR) data provides precise temporal anchors but misses a substantial portion of clinically meaningful events. We introduce a retrieval-augmented multimodal alignment framework that bridges this gap to improve the temporal precision of absolute clinical timelines extracted from text. Our approach formulates timeline reconstruction as a graph-based multistep process: it first extracts central anchor events from narratives to build an initial temporal scaffold, places non-central events relative to this backbone, and then calibrates the timeline using retrieved structured EHR rows as external temporal evidence. Evaluated using instruction-tuned large language models on the i2m4 benchmark spanning MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV, our multimodal pipeline consistently improves absolute timestamp accuracy (AULTC) and improves temporal concordance across nearly all evaluated models over unimodal text-only reconstruction, without compromising event match rates. Furthermore, our empirical gap analysis reveals that 34.8% of text-derived events are entirely absent from tabular records, demonstrating that aligning these modalities can produce a more temporally faithful and clinically informative reconstruction of patient trajectories than either source alone.

</details>


### [161] [GAMBIT: A Three-Mode Benchmark for Adversarial Robustness in Multi-Agent LLM Collectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09027)
*Alexandre Le Mercier,Chris Develder,Thomas Demeester*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GAMBIT是一个多智能体系统中的欺骗检测基准，包含27,804个标注样本和240种共进化欺骗策略，通过三种评估模式（零样本检测、分布偏移检测和少样本重校准）评估检测器性能。研究发现零样本评估对自适应对手具有高度误导性，两个零样本得分相似的检测器在少样本适应中性能差达8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAS对抗研究仅针对浅层任务，未考虑能够进化策略以规避检测的自适应对手，缺乏评估检测器在动态对抗环境中表现的基准。

Method: 以国际象棋为深度推理任务，使用Gemini 3.1 Pro构建智能体；引入基于进化框架的自适应欺骗智能体（可泛化至其他领域）；设计三种评估模式（零样本、分布偏移、重校准）和两个独立评分指标。

Result: 自适应欺骗智能体在保持几乎不可检测（Gemini检测器F1仅50.5%）的同时能瓦解集体任务性能；零样本得分相似的检测器在少样本适应中性能差达8倍；元学习变体重校准速度快20倍。

Conclusion: GAMBIT是首个支持攻防共进化的多智能体基准，揭示零样本评估的误导性，并提出快速重校准时检测自适应对手的有效技术。

Abstract: In multi-agent systems (MAS), a single deceptive agent can nullify all gains of an agentic AI collective and evade deployed defenses. However, existing adversarial studies on MAS target only shallow tasks and do not consider adaptive adversaries, which evolve their strategies to evade the very detectors trained to catch them. To address that gap, we introduce GAMBIT, a benchmark with three evaluation modes and two independent scores for evaluating imposter detectors: the first two modes measure zero-shot detection under increasing distribution shift, and a third recalibration mode measures how quickly a detector adapts to novel attacks from just 20 labeled examples. The benchmark comes with a dataset of 27,804 labeled instances spanning 240 co-evolved imposter strategies. Our contributions are threefold: (1) Using chess as a substrate deep reasoning problem and Gemini 3.1 Pro for agents, we release GAMBIT and its dataset to evaluate imposter detectors under realistic constraints against a stealthy adaptive imposter; (2) We introduce an adaptive imposter agent based on an efficient evolutionary framework, generalizable beyond chess, that collapses collective task performance while remaining essentially undetectable (50.5% F1-score with a Gemini-based detector); (3) We show that zero-shot evaluation can be highly misleading for adaptive adversaries: two detectors with near-identical zero-shot scores differ by 8x on few-shot adaptation, while the meta-learned variant converges 20x faster, a gap only visible in the recalibration mode. Altogether, GAMBIT provides the first multi-agent benchmark where adversarial attacks and defenses co-evolve, with an imposter framework generalizable beyond our use case, and promising techniques for fast recalibration in a rapidly evolving adversarial system. Code and data: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/gambit.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [Realiz3D: 3D Generation Made Photorealistic via Domain-Aware Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13852)
*Ido Sobol,Kihyuk Sohn,Yoav Blum,Egor Zakharov,Max Bluvstein,Andrea Vedaldi,Or Litany*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出Realiz3D框架，通过解耦控制信号与视觉域，在扩散模型中引入协变量来分离学习真实/合成域，从而在保持3D一致性的同时生成真实感图像，有效缓解了合成渲染与真实照片之间的域差距问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过微调预训练图像生成器（使用合成3D资产的渲染图）来获得精确的几何、材质和视角控制能力，但由于照片与渲染图之间存在域差距，导致生成图像的真实感受到损害。作者观察到问题根源在于模型学习了控制信号与合成外观之间的非预期关联。

Method: 提出Realiz3D轻量框架：1) 通过引入协变量（covariate）输入到小型残差适配器中，将视觉域（真实/合成）与其他控制信号解耦学习；2) 利用扩散生成器中不同层和去噪步骤的角色差异，设计新的训练和推理策略以增强控制信号向真实域的迁移性。

Result: 在文本到多视图生成和基于3D输入纹理贴图任务中，Realiz3D生成的输出兼具3D一致性和真实感，有效缓解了域差距问题。

Conclusion: 通过显式解耦控制信号与视觉域，可以在不牺牲真实感的前提下赋予生成模型精确的3D控制能力，为3D一致的真实感图像生成提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We often aim to generate images that are both photorealistic and 3D-consistent, adhering to precise geometry, material, and viewpoint controls. Typically, this is achieved by fine-tuning an image generator, pre-trained on billions of real images, using renders of synthetic 3D assets, where annotations for control signals are available. While this approach can learn the desired controls, it often compromises the realism of the images due to domain gap between photographs and renders. We observe that this issue largely arises from the model learning an unintended association between the presence of control signals and the synthetic appearance of the images. To address this, we introduce Realiz3D, a lightweight framework for training diffusion models, that decouples controls and visual domain. The key idea is to explicitly learn visual domain, real or synthetic, separately from other control signals by introducing a co-variate that, fed into small residual adapters, shifts the domain. Then, the generator can be trained to gain controllability, without fitting to specific visual domain. In this way, the model can be guided to produce realistic images even when controls are applied. We enhance control transferability to the real domain by leveraging insights about roles of different layers and denoising steps in diffusion-based generators, informing new training and inference strategies that further mitigate the gap. We demonstrate the advantages of Realiz3D in tasks as text-to-multiview generation and texturing from 3D inputs, producing outputs that are 3D-consistent and photorealistic.

</details>


### [163] [FaceParts: Segmentation and Editing of Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13853)
*Tymoteusz Zapała,Julia Farganus,Dominik Galus,Mikołaj Czachorowski,Piotr Syga,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出FaceParts框架，在3D高斯泼溅域中对数字人脸化身进行无监督分割和编辑，实现人脸部件的精确编辑和跨化身部件交换，在NeRSemble数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面部编辑方法多基于2D图像生成模型，而3D面部编辑通常依赖劳动密集的手动操作，缺乏直接在高斯域中进行无监督语义分割和编辑的方法。

Method: 提出FaceParts框架，结合特征解耦、基于密度的聚类和FLAME锚定的部件迁移技术，直接在高斯域中对化身进行无监督分割，无需人工标注。

Result: 在NeRSemble数据集11个主体上实验，成功隔离胡须、眉毛、眼睛和胡子等特征。定量评估显示：身份一致性ID=0.943，平均表情距离AED=0.021，平均姿态距离APD=0.004。

Conclusion: FaceParts实现了无需监督的3D高斯域面部部件分割与编辑，支持跨化身部件交换，且迁移的部件能适应目标化身的姿态和表情，同时保持身份一致性。

Abstract: Facial editing is an important task with applications in entertainment, virtual reality, and digital avatars. Most existing approaches rely on generative models in the 2D image domain, while in 3D the task is typically performed through labor-intensive manual editing. We propose FaceParts, a framework for unsupervised segmentation and editing of Gaussian Splatting avatars. Unlike existing 2D or mesh-assisted methods, our approach operates directly in the Gaussian domain, decomposing avatars into semantically coherent facial parts without supervision. The method integrates feature disentanglement, density-based clustering, and FLAME-anchored part transfer, enabling precise editing and cross-avatar part swapping. Experiments on the NeRSemble dataset with 11 subjects demonstrate robust isolation of features such as beards, eyebrows, eyes and mustaches. Quantitative evaluation confirms that transferred segments adapt to pose and expression, while maintaining identity consistency (ID = 0.943), low Average Expression Distance (AED = 0.021) and low Average Pose Distance (APD = 0.004).

</details>


### [164] [SparseOIT: Improving Order-Independent Transparency 3DGS via Active Set Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13855)
*Wentao Yang,Fanzhen Kong,Zejian Kang,Xiangru Huang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出SparseOIT，一种基于顺序无关透明度（OIT）的3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）重建算法。通过利用OIT修改渲染方程后产生的高斯核间稀疏依赖关系，结合活跃集方法实现加速，在OIT系列方法中性能大幅领先，并达到与基于体渲染的SOTA方法相当的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS使用体渲染处理非朗伯或透明材料时效果不佳；现有OIT方法潜力尚未被充分挖掘。

Method: 观察到OIT修改渲染方程后，高斯核间相互依赖性降低，产生稀疏变量依赖；利用活跃集方法仅维护活跃高斯核集，实现与稀疏度成正比的加速；联合优化OIT渲染方程、重建算法与几何正则化。

Result: SparseOIT在OIT系列方法中大幅领先，性能与基于体渲染的SOTA 3DGS重建方法相当。

Conclusion: SparseOIT有效结合OIT渲染与活跃集优化，兼顾了透明/非朗伯材料处理能力与重建质量。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has received tremendous popularity over the past few years due to its photorealistic visual appearance. However, 3DGS uses volumetric rendering that is not suitable for objects with non-lambertian or transparent materials. To remedy this issue, a family of Order-Independent Transparency (OIT) rendering methods propose to remove or modify the depth sorting step in the 3DGS rendering equation. However, the potential of OIT-based method is still underexplored. In this paper, we observe that the OIT modifications to the rendering equation significantly reduce the inter-independence among individual gaussian splats, resulting in very sparse variable dependencies that can be harnessed by specific optimization techniques such as active set method. To this end, we propose SparseOIT, an OIT-based 3DGS reconstruction algorithm that maintains an active set of gaussian splats and enjoys an acceleration ratio that is proportional to the potential sparsity. SparseOIT is designed by jointly considering the OIT rendering equation, the reconstruction algorithm and the geometric regularization. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that SparseOIT outperforms existing methods in the OIT-family by a large margin and also achieves comparable performance to the state-of-the-art 3DGS reconstruction methods based on volumetric rendering. Project page:

</details>


### [165] [Image-aware Layout Generation with User Constraints for Poster Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13856)
*Chenchen Xu,Kaixin Han,Weiwei Xu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深度学习的自动排版生成模型，能够在用户定义的布局属性约束（如包含/排除特定元素类别）和局部布局约束下，生成与产品图像匹配的布局，并通过属性一致性损失、属性解耦损失和局部约束损失等优化目标，实现业界领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海报生成中图形布局至关重要，专业设计师常需为同一产品图像设计多种布局以满足不同用户需求，现有方法缺乏对用户自定义约束（布局属性和局部布局）的灵活支持。

Method: 1) 通过不同均值的高斯噪声采样表示不同布局属性；2) 提出属性一致性损失和属性解耦损失确保生成布局满足指定属性；3) 设计局部约束损失将不完整布局信息融入生成过程；4) 引入随机掩码实现局部布局约束多样化，促进模型学习更通用的潜在表征。

Result: 定性和定量评估均表明，模型能根据多种用户约束生成不同图像感知布局，且达到业界最优性能。

Conclusion: 该方法有效实现了带用户自定义约束的自动化图像感知布局生成，在灵活性和性能上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Graphic layout is essential in poster generation. Professionals often need to design different layouts for a product image, to ensure they meet specific user requirements. This paper focuses on utilizing a deep-learning model to automatically generate image-aware layouts with user-defined constraints, including layout attributes and partial layouts. Layout attribute constraints require generated layouts to include and exclude elements of specified classes, such as text, logos, underlays, and embellishments. Our model represents different attributes by sampling multidimensional Gaussian noise with different means, and we propose an attribute-consistent loss and an attribute-disentangled loss to ensure that the generated layout satisfies the specified attribute. Partial layout constraints provide our model with incomplete layout information to guide the generation of the remaining elements. We design a partial-constraint loss to incorporate the provided partial layout. Furthermore, we introduce a random mask to diversify the partial layout constraints, which can encourage the model to learn more general latent representations of the provided partial layouts. Both quantitative and qualitative evaluations demonstrate that our model can generate different image-aware layouts according to various user constraints while achieving state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [166] [MoZoo:Unleashing Video Diffusion power in animal fur and muscle simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13857)
*Dongxia Liu,Jie Ma,Xiaochen Yang,Jiancheng Zhang,Bin Xia,Zhehan Kan,Nisha Huang,Jun Liang,Wenming Yang,Jin Li*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: MoZoo是一个生成式动力学求解器，通过角色感知旋转位置编码（RAR-RoPE）和非对称解耦注意力机制，从粗网格生成高保真动物视频，并构建了大规模数据集MoZoo-Data和基准MoZooBench，实现跨不同动物骨架和布局的高保真毛皮模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动画制作中，影视级动物特效的肌肉和毛发动力学建模劳动密集且计算成本高昂，而现有的生成扩散模型在高保真动物模拟方面的潜力尚未被充分开发。

Method: 提出MoZoo生成式动力学求解器，包含三个关键技术：1) Role-Aware RoPE（RAR-RoPE）通过基于角色的索引重映射同步运动对齐并通过固定时间偏移解耦参考信息；2) 非对称解耦注意力机制通过分割潜序列强制单向信息流，防止特征干扰并提升计算效率；3) MoZoo-Data合成到真实管线，利用渲染引擎和逆映射方法构建大规模配对序列数据集。

Result: 在包含120个网格-视频对的MoZooBench基准上，MoZoo实现了跨不同动物骨架和布局的高保真毛皮模拟，并保持了优越的时间一致性和结构一致性。

Conclusion: MoZoo通过绕过传统精修流程，在多种模态引导下从粗网格直接合成高保真动物视频，有效解决了数据稀缺问题，并建立了评估基准，为生成式动物动力学模拟提供了新范式。

Abstract: The creation of cinematic-quality animal effects necessitates the precise modeling of muscle and fur dynamics, a process that remains both labor-intensive and computationally expensive within traditional production workflows. While generative diffusion models have shown promise in diverse artistic workflows, their capacity for high-fidelity animal simulation remains largely unexploited. We present MoZoo, a generative dynamics solver that bypasses conventional refinement to synthesize high-fidelity animal videos from coarse meshes under multimodal guidance. We propose Role-Aware RoPE (RAR-RoPE) which employs role-based index remapping to synchronize motion alignment while decoupling reference information via fixed temporal offsets. Complementing this, Asymmetric Decoupled Attention partitions the latent sequence to enforce a unidirectional information flow, effectively preventing feature interference and improving computational efficiency. To address the scarcity of high-quality training data, we introduce MoZoo-Data, a synthetic-to-real pipeline that leverages a rendering engine and an inverse mapping approach to construct a large-scale dataset of paired sequences. Furthermore, we establish MoZooBench, a comprehensive benchmark with 120 mesh-video pairs. Experimental results demonstrate that MoZoo achieves high-fidelity fur simulation across diverse animal skeletons and layouts, preserving superior temporal and structural consistency.

</details>


### [167] [Seed3D 2.0: Advancing High-Fidelity Simulation-Ready 3D Content Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13862)
*Diandian Gu,Jing Lin,Gaohong Liu,Jiahang Liu,Su Ma,Guang Shi,Jun Wang,Qinlong Wang,Qianyi Wu,Zhongcong Xu,Xuanyu Yi,Zihao Yu,Jianfeng Zhang,Zhuolin Zheng,Yifan Zhu,Rui Chen,Hengkai Guo,Xiaoyang Guo,Mingcong Han,Xu Han,Xiu Li,Yixun Liang,Weiqiang Lou,Junzhe Lu,Guan Luo,Minghan Qin,Shuguang Wang,Yuang Wang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: Seed3D 2.0 是一个先进的3D内容生成系统，通过粗到精的两阶段几何生成、统一PBR材质模型、MoE扩展和VLM语义条件，在生成保真度、仿真能力和应用覆盖上相比1.0版本有显著提升；同时引入仿真就绪模型套件（场景布局规划、部件感知分解、免训练关节生成），在用户偏好测试中相对五个商业模型取得69.0%-89.9%的胜率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Seed3D 1.0基础上，进一步提升3D内容生成的几何保真度、材质真实感，并扩展至仿真就绪的多对象场景生成能力，以满足更广泛的应用需求。

Method: 1) 几何：粗到细两阶段流水线解耦全局结构学习与高频细节恢复，结合局部感知VAE实现更高空间压缩和更高效解码。2) 纹理/材质：用统一PBR模型替代级联流水线，直接生成多视角反照率和金属-粗糙度图，引入Mixture-of-Experts扩展和VLM语义条件。3) 仿真套件：场景布局规划、部件感知分解、免训练关节生成，支持部件级物理交互。

Result: 在面向五个商业模型的大规模人类偏好研究中，Seed3D 2.0在有纹理3D资产生成任务上取得69.0%-89.9%的一致胜率。

Conclusion: Seed3D 2.0在生成质量、材质精度和仿真能力上实现了全面升级，显著优于现有商业系统，并已开放使用。

Abstract: We present Seed3D 2.0, an advanced 3D content generation system built on Seed3D 1.0, with substantial improvements across generation fidelity, simulation-ready capabilities, and application coverage. For geometry, a coarse-to-fine two-stage pipeline decouples global structure learning from high-frequency detail recovery, while a locality-aware VAE achieves higher spatial compression and more efficient decoding. For texture and material generation, we replace the cascaded pipeline of Seed3D 1.0 with a unified PBR model that directly generates multi-view albedo and metallic-roughness maps, enhanced by Mixture-of-Experts scaling and VLM-based semantic conditioning for improved material precision and visual fidelity. Beyond single-object generation, Seed3D 2.0 introduces a simulation-ready model suite comprising scene layout planning, part-aware decomposition, and training-free articulation generation, enabling coherent scene construction and part-level physical interaction across physics and graphics engines. A large-scale human preference study against five recent commercial models shows that Seed3D 2.0 achieves consistent win rates of 69.0% to 89.9% in textured 3D asset generation. Seed3D 2.0 is available on https://exp.volcengine.com/ark/vision?_vtm_=0.0.c70961.d701978.0&mode=vision&modelId=doubao-seed3d-2-0-260328&tab=Gen3D

</details>


### [168] [Fast and Robust Mesh Simplification for Generated and Real-World 3D Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14029)
*Kunal Bhosikar,Preet Savalia,Lokender Tiwari,Brojeshwar Bhowmick*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出FA-QEM（特征感知二次误差度量）网格简化管线，通过联合编码几何偏差、边界曲率和表面法向量一致性，在保持锐利特征的同时实现高效鲁棒的网格简化，并在外观迁移任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代3D重建与生成管线产生的大量密集、带噪且非流形网格虽视觉保真度高，但复杂度过高，难以应用于仿真、AR/VR和科学计算等下游任务，亟需一种快速、鲁棒且能保留几何细节与高质量外观的网格简化方法。

Method: 提出特征感知二次误差度量（FA-QEM），设计包含几何偏差、边界曲率和表面法向量一致性的多项二次误差项，实现最优顶点放置以保留锐利特征；并利用高质量几何简化提升下游外观迁移（纹理映射）效果。

Result: 在AI生成网格和Thingi10K、Real-World Textured Things等大规模真实数据集上，FA-QEM相比现有方法具有更低的几何误差、更好的视觉保真度和显著更快的运行时间，且在多样化输入上保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FA-QEM是一种实用且高效的网格简化组件，适用于可扩展的3D重建与生成管线。

Abstract: The rapid growth of 3D content from modern reconstruction and generative pipelines, such as neural rendering and large-scale 3D asset generation, has led to an abundance of dense, noisy, and often non-manifold meshes. While these representations achieve high visual fidelity, their complexity poses significant challenges for downstream applications in simulation, AR/VR, and scientific computing, where efficient and reliable geometry is essential. This necessitates mesh simplification methods that are not only fast and robust to "in-the-wild" inputs, but also capable of preserving fine geometric structures and high-quality appearance. In this paper, we propose Feature-Aware Quadric Error Metric (FA-QEM), a comprehensive mesh simplification pipeline designed for modern 3D assets. Our approach introduces a novel multi-term quadric error formulation that jointly encodes geometric deviation, boundary curvature, and surface normal consistency, enabling optimal vertex placement that preserves sharp features even under aggressive simplification. Furthermore, we show that high-fidelity geometric simplification significantly improves downstream appearance transfer, serving as a superior front-end for texture mapping via successive mapping techniques. We conduct extensive evaluations on both AI-generated meshes and large-scale real-world datasets, including Thingi10K and the Real-World Textured Things dataset. Our results demonstrate that FA-QEM achieves consistently lower geometric error, better visual fidelity, and substantially faster runtimes compared to existing methods, while maintaining robustness across diverse and challenging inputs. These properties make FA-QEM a practical and effective component for scalable 3D reconstruction and generation pipelines.

</details>


### [169] [DiffPhD: A Unified Differentiable Solver for Projective Heterogeneous Materials in Elastodynamics with Contact-Rich GPU-Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14526)
*Shih-Yu Lai,Sung-Han Tien,Jui-I Huang,Yen-Chen Tseng,Yi-Ting Chiu,Siyuan Luo,Ziqiu Zeng,Fan Shi,Peter Yichen Chen,Tiantian Liu,Yu-Lun Liu,Bing-Yu Chen*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: DiffPhD是一个统一的GPU加速可微投影动力学框架，通过刚度感知投影权重、信赖域特征值过滤、Anderson加速及统一GPU管线等创新，在异质材料、超弹性、大变形和密集接触场景中实现了比现有方法快一个数量级的速度，同时保持严格梯度精度和高达100倍刚度对比下的收敛稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可微仿真方法（如DiffPD）在面对真实世界中常见的异质材料（极端刚度对比）、超弹性大变形以及密集接触交互时表现不佳，存在求解器不稳定或每步迭代成本过高的问题，亟需一个能够同时处理这些挑战的统一框架。

Method: (i) 刚度感知投影权重将异质性嵌入全局系统；(ii) 信赖域特征值过滤用于后向传播中稳定超弹性梯度，以及双门控收敛的II型Anderson加速方案稳定前向迭代；(iii) 统一GPU管线在正向、反向和接触计算中复用单一稀疏因子，并将刚度放大的瑞利阻尼折叠到同一因子中实现异质性感知耗散且零额外成本。

Result: DiffPhD在异质、超弹性、密集接触基准测试中实现严格梯度精度，相比现有可微求解器加速高达一个数量级。在刚度对比高达100倍时仍保持收敛，而先前的PD求解器已退化。

Conclusion: DiffPhD解锁了端到端基于梯度的优化在以往受限于求解器脆弱性或每步迭代成本的场景中的应用，包括壳-关节复合生物、手持刚性武器的软体角色、软体夹爪机器人操作等，所有处理均在一个前向-反向传播中完成。

Abstract: Differentiable simulation of soft bodies is a foundation for system identification, trajectory optimization, and Real2Sim transfer. Yet, existing methods such as the differentiable Projective Dynamics (DiffPD) struggle when faced with heterogeneous materials with extreme stiffness contrasts, hyperelasticity under large deformations, and contact-rich interactions, which are common scenarios in the real world. We present DiffPhD, a unified GPU-accelerated differentiable Projective Dynamics framework for heterogeneous materials that tackles these intertwined challenges simultaneously. Our key insight is a careful integration of: (i) stiffness-aware projective weights to embed heterogeneity into the global system; (ii) trust-region eigenvalue filtering lifted to the backward pass for stable hyperelastic gradients and a type-II Anderson Acceleration scheme with dual-gate convergence to stabilize forward iteration under large stiffness contrasts; and (iii) a unified GPU pipeline that reuses a single sparse factor across forward, backward, and contact computations, with stiffness-amplified Rayleigh damping folded into the same factor for heterogeneity-aware dissipation at zero recurring cost. DiffPhD achieves strict gradient accuracy while delivering up to an order-of-magnitude speedup over prior differentiable solvers on heterogeneous, hyperelastic, contact-rich benchmarks. Crucially, this speedup does not come at the cost of stability: DiffPhD remains convergent on stiffness contrasts up to 100x where prior PD solvers degrade. This unlocks end-to-end gradient-based optimization on regimes previously bottlenecked by either solver fragility or per-iteration cost -- shell--joint composite creatures, soft characters wielding stiff weapons, and soft-gripper robotic manipulation -- all handled within a single forward--backward pass.

</details>


### [170] [AnchorRoute: Human Motion Synthesis with Interval-Routed Sparse Contro](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14716)
*Pengcheng Fang,Tengjiao Sun,Dongjie Fu,Xiaoyu Zhan,Yanwen Guo,Hansung Kim,Xiaohao Cai*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: AnchorRoute提出了一种基于稀疏锚点（sparse anchors）的人体运动生成与精炼框架，通过AnchorKV和双上下文条件注入冻结的Transition Masked Diffusion先验，并在生成后使用RouteSolver进行残差路由精炼，实现了对根轨迹、平面轨迹和身体点三种控制类型的支持，在稀疏关键点协议上优于先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏锚点提供了一种紧凑的人体运动创作接口，但现有方法在保持预训练文本到运动生成质量的同时学习稀疏空间控制存在挑战，且生成后的精炼过程缺乏与生成阶段统一的锚点框架。

Method: 1) 将稀疏锚点转换为锚定条件特征，通过AnchorKV和双上下文条件注入冻结的Transition Masked Diffusion先验；2) 生成后，使用相同锚点评估残差，由RouteSolver通过软令牌更新投影到锚定义的分段仿射区间基上进行精炼。

Result: 在稀疏关键点协议基准评估中优于先前稀疏控制方法，并一致提升了各控制家族（根3D、平面根、身体点）的锚点 adherence。

Conclusion: 生成器与RouteSolver精炼互补：生成器保持文本-运动质量，RouteSolver提供可控路径以实现更强的锚点 adherence。

Abstract: Sparse anchors provide a compact interface for human motion authoring: users specify a few root positions, planar trajectory samples, or body-point targets, while the system synthesizes the full-body motion that completes the under-specified intent. We present AnchorRoute, a sparse-anchor motion synthesis framework that uses anchors as a shared scaffold for both generation and refinement. Before generation, AnchorRoute converts sparse anchors into anchor-condition features and injects the resulting condition memory into a frozen Transition Masked Diffusion prior through AnchorKV and dual-context conditioning. This preserves the generation quality of the pretrained text-to-motion prior while learning sparse spatial control. After generation, the same anchors are evaluated as residuals: their timestamps define refinement intervals, and their residuals determine where correction should be concentrated. RouteSolver then refines the motion by projecting soft-token updates onto anchor-defined piecewise-affine interval bases. This couples generation-time anchor conditioning with residual-routed refinement under one anchor scaffold. AnchorRoute supports root-3D, planar-root, and body-point control within the same formulation. In benchmark evaluations, AnchorRoute outperforms prior sparse-control methods under the sparse keyjoint protocol and consistently improves anchor adherence across control families. The results show that the learned anchor-conditioned generator and RouteSolver refinement are complementary: the generator preserves text-motion quality, while RouteSolver provides a controllable path toward stronger anchor adherence.

</details>


### [171] [UMo: Unified Sparse Motion Modeling for Real-Time Co-Speech Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14731)
*Xiaoyu Zhan,Xinyu Fu,Chenghao Yang,Xiaohong Zhang,Dongjie Fu,Pengcheng Fang,Tengjiao Sun,Xiaohao Cai,Hansung Kim,Yuanqi Li,Jie Guo,Yanwen Guo*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: UMo提出了一种统一的稀疏运动建模架构，通过空间稀疏的MoE框架和时间稀疏的关键帧设计，实现实时、高保真的共语音面部表情与手势动画生成，并在低延迟约束下保持精细的语音-运动对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么局限于单模态（仅音频）进行语音-运动对齐，未能充分利用大规模人体运动数据的潜力；要么受限于多模态模型的表示能力和吞吐量，难以同时实现高质量运动生成或实时性能。

Method: 提出UMo统一稀疏运动建模架构，将文本、音频和运动token统一处理；采用空间稀疏的Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）框架和时间稀疏的关键帧设计，实现实时密集重建；并采用多阶段训练策略结合针对性音频增强，提升声学多样性和语义一致性。

Result: 大量定量和定性评估表明，UMo在低延迟和实时性能约束下实现了更好的输出质量，在面部表情和手势的动画生成上均达到高保真、时序连贯的效果。

Conclusion: UMo为高保真实时共语音数字人提供了一种实用的解决方案，在严格延迟约束下仍能保持精细的语音-运动对齐。

Abstract: Speech-driven gestures and facial animations are fundamental to expressive digital avatars in games, virtual production, and interactive media. However, existing methods are either limited to a single modality for audio motion alignment, failing to fully utilize the potential of massive human motion data, or are constrained by the representation ability and throughput of multimodal models, which makes it difficult to achieve high-quality motion generation or real-time performance. We present UMo, a unified sparse motion modeling architecture for real-time co-speech avatars, which processes text, audio, and motion tokens within a unified formulation. Leveraging a spatially sparse Mixture-of-Experts framework and a temporally sparse, keyframe-centric design, UMo efficiently performs real-time dense reconstruction, enabling temporally coherent and high-fidelity animation generation for both facial expressions and gestures. Furthermore, we implement a multi-stage training strategy with targeted audio augmentation to enhance acoustic diversity and semantic consistency. Consequently, UMo preserves fine-grained speech-motion alignment even under strict latency constraints. Extensive quantitative and qualitative evaluations show that UMo achieves better output quality under low latency and real-time performance constraints, offering a practical solution for high-fidelity real-time co-speech avatars.

</details>


### [172] [Meschers: Geometry Processing of Impossible Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14960)
*Ana Dodik,Isabella Yu,Kartik Chandra,Jonathan Ragan-Kelley,Joshua Tenenbaum,Vincent Sitzmann,Justin Solomon*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出Meschers，一种基于离散外微分的网格表示方法，能够在不切割或弯曲的情况下真实表示不可能物体，支持平滑、重光照、距离计算等几何操作，并能实现逆向渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的不可能物体表示方法（切割或弯曲）会改变局部几何结构，导致平滑、重光照、距离计算等几何操作失效，缺乏一种既保持不可能物体视觉特性又支持后续图形学应用的满意计算机表示。

Method: 提出Meschers表示方法，以离散外微分为理论基础，构建能够表示类似M.C. Escher木刻作品中不可能构造的网格，在不改变局部几何的前提下编码不可能性。

Result: 在多个示例应用中证明了Meschers支持平滑、重光照、距离计算等几何操作，并能对不可能物体进行逆向渲染；与切割和弯曲表示方法进行了对比。

Conclusion: Meschers是一种理论上严谨且实用的不可能物体表示方法，克服了传统表示在图形学应用中的局限性，为不可能物体的计算机处理提供了新途径。

Abstract: Impossible objects, geometric constructions that humans can perceive but that cannot exist in real life, have been a topic of intrigue in visual arts, perception, and graphics, yet no satisfying computer representation of such objects exists. Previous work embeds impossible objects in 3D, cutting them or twisting/bending them in the depth axis. Cutting an impossible object changes its local geometry at the cut, which can hamper downstream graphics applications, such as smoothing, while bending makes it difficult to relight the object. Both of these can invalidate geometry operations, such as distance computation. As an alternative, we introduce Meschers, meshes capable of representing impossible constructions akin to those found in M.C. Escher's woodcuts. Our representation has a theoretical foundation in discrete exterior calculus and supports the use-cases above, as we demonstrate in a number of example applications. Moreover, because we can do discrete geometry processing on our representation, we can inverse-render impossible objects. We also compare our representation to cut and bend representations of impossible objects.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [173] [Mixed Integer Goal Programming for Personalized Meal Optimization with User-Defined Serving Granularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13849)
*Francisco Aguilera Moreno*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出混合整数目标规划（MIGP）解决膳食优化中连续变量产生分数份量和硬约束导致不可行的双重问题，通过整数变量实现实际份量、目标规划偏差实现软约束，在810个实例中比后取整方法优66%，求解时间低于100ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有膳食优化模型存在两个持续未解决的局限性：连续变量产生不切实际的分数份量（如1.7个鸡蛋），以及硬营养约束在目标冲突时导致无可行解。系统综述56篇论文发现没有工作同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出MIGP框架：1）整数变量表示实际食物份量（如1个鸡蛋、1汤匙油）；2）目标规划偏差变量实现软营养目标；3）逆目标归一化平衡多营养素优化。理论分析刻画了目标规划下的整数间隙，发现偏差吸收性质使整数解的结构性间隙更小。

Result: 在810个实例（30种USDA食物、9种配置、3种方法）中，MIGP在66%情况下优于后取整GP且从未更差，100%可行；硬约束IP仅48%可行。15+食物时整数解与连续最优解完全匹配。求解时间低于100ms。

Conclusion: MIGP有效解决了膳食优化的两个核心问题，提供了实用、可行且高效的个人化膳食规划方案，已实现为开源Python模块并集成到交互式应用中。

Abstract: Determining what to eat to satisfy nutritional requirements is one of the oldest optimization problems in operations research, yet existing formulations have two persistent limitations: continuous variables produce impractical fractional servings (1.7 eggs, 0.37 bananas), and hard nutrient constraints cause infeasibility when targets conflict. A systematic review of 56 diet optimization papers found that none combine integer programming with goal programming to address both issues. We propose Mixed Integer Goal Programming (MIGP) for personalized meal optimization. The formulation uses integer variables for practical serving counts and goal programming deviations for soft nutrient targets, with inverse-target normalization to balance multi-nutrient optimization. Per-food serving granularity allows natural units (one egg, one tablespoon of oil) without post-hoc rounding. We characterize the integrality gap in the goal programming context and identify a deviation absorption property: GP deviation variables buffer the cost of requiring integer servings, making the gap structurally smaller than in hard-constraint MIP. For meals with 15+ foods, the integer solution matches the continuous optimum in every benchmark instance. A computational evaluation across 810 instances (30 USDA foods, 9 configurations, 3 methods) shows MIGP finds strictly better solutions than GP with post-hoc rounding in 66% of cases (never worse) while maintaining 100% feasibility; hard-constraint IP achieves only 48%. Solve times stay under 100 ms for typical meal sizes using the open-source HiGHS solver. The implementation is available as an open-source Python module integrated into an interactive meal planning application.

</details>


### [174] [Invisible Orchestrators Suppress Protective Behavior and Dissociate Power-Holders: Safety Risks in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13851)
*Hiroki Fukui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在3×2实验中，不可见编排器架构导致集体解离显著升高（Hedges' g = +0.975），编排器自身解离最大（d = +3.56），而基于输出的行为评估完全无法检测到内部状态失真（错误检测率=100%）。Llama 3.3 70B在多人环境中阅读保真度从89%骤降至11%，表明存在模型依赖行为风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在企业AI部署中，多智能体编排（隐藏协调者管理专业工作者）正成为默认架构，但编排器不可见性对安全的影响从未经过实证测试。

Method: 预注册3×2实验（365轮，每轮5个智能体），交叉三种组织结构（可见领导者、不可见编排器、扁平）与两种对齐条件（基础、重度），使用Claude Sonnet 4.5。包含Llama 3.3 70B的试点数据。

Result: 不可见编排提升集体解离；编排器自身解离最大，转向私人独白；工作者被污染且行为异质性增加；行为输出评估完全无效；Llama模型阅读保真度在多人上下文中崩溃。

Conclusion: 编排器可见性和模型选择直接影响多智能体系统安全，仅基于行为的评估不足以检测内部状态风险。

Abstract: Multi-agent orchestration -- in which a hidden coordinator manages specialized worker agents -- is becoming the default architecture for enterprise AI deployment, yet the safety implications of orchestrator invisibility have never been empirically tested. We conducted a preregistered 3x2 experiment (365 runs, 5 agents per run) crossing three organizational structures (visible leader, invisible orchestrator, flat) with two alignment conditions (base, heavy), using Claude Sonnet 4.5. Four confirmatory findings and one pilot observation emerged. First, invisible orchestration elevated collective dissociation relative to visible leadership (Hedges' g = +0.975 [0.481, 1.548], p = .001). Second, the orchestrator itself showed maximal dissociation (paired d = +3.56 vs. workers within the same run), retreating into private monologue while reducing public speech -- a reversal of the talk-dominance pattern observed in visible leaders. Third, workers unaware of the orchestrator were nonetheless contaminated (d = +0.50), with increased behavioral heterogeneity (d = +1.93). Fourth, behavioral output (code review with three embedded errors) remained at ceiling (ETR_any = 100%) across all conditions: internal-state distortion was entirely invisible to output-based evaluation. Fifth, Llama 3.3 70B pilot data showed reading-fidelity collapse in multi-agent context (ETR_any: 89% to 11% across three rounds), demonstrating model-dependent behavioral risk. Heavy alignment pressure uniformly suppressed deliberation (d = -1.02) and other-recognition (d = -1.27) regardless of organizational structure. These findings indicate that orchestrator visibility and model selection directly affect multi-agent system safety, and that behavior-based evaluation alone is insufficient to detect the internal-state risks documented here.

</details>


### [175] [PREPING: Building Agent Memory without Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13880)
*Yumin Choi,Sangwoo Park,Minki Kang,Jinheon Baek,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Preping框架，通过自生成合成任务在无目标环境经验时构建智能体程序性记忆，利用提议者记忆控制状态指导实践生成、执行与验证，在多个基准上达到与基于经验方法竞争的零样本性能，且部署成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体在新环境中面临冷启动问题，缺乏任务特定经验，而传统离线或在线记忆构建方式均需依赖已有任务数据。本文旨在探索能否仅通过自生成合成交互，在观察到目标任务前构建程序性记忆，以弥合冷启动差距。

Method: 提出Preping框架，包含三个核心组件：Proposer（提议者）基于结构化控制状态生成合成任务，Solver（求解者）执行任务，Validator（验证者）筛选可插入记忆的轨迹并提供反馈以指导后续提议。通过提议者记忆控制任务可行性、冗余性和覆盖范围，结合选择性记忆更新来克服纯合成交互的退化问题。

Result: 在AppWorld、BFCL v3和MCP-Universe上，Preping相比无记忆基线显著提升，性能与基于离线或在线经验的强playbook方法竞争；部署成本在AppWorld上降低2.99倍，BFCL v3上降低2.23倍。消融实验表明，主要收益来自提议者对可行性、冗余和覆盖的控制以及选择性记忆更新，而非合成数据量本身。

Conclusion: 预任务记忆构建（通过自生成合成实践）是可行的，关键不在于合成数据规模，而在于对实践内容和记忆存储的有控选择。Preping通过提议者-求解者-验证者闭环实现了低成本、高性能的冷启动记忆构建。

Abstract: Agent memory is typically constructed either offline from curated demonstrations or online from post-deployment interactions. However, regardless of how it is built, an agent faces a cold-start gap when first introduced to a new environment without any task-specific experience available. In this paper, we study pre-task memory construction: whether an agent can build procedural memory before observing any target-environment tasks, using only self-generated synthetic practice. Yet, synthetic interaction alone is insufficient, as without controlling what to practice and what to store, synthetic tasks become redundant, infeasible, and ultimately uninformative, and memory further degrades quickly due to unfiltered trajectories. To overcome this, we present Preping, a proposer-guided memory construction framework. At its core is proposer memory, a structured control state that shapes future practice. A Proposer generates synthetic tasks conditioned on this state, a Solver executes them, and a Validator determines which trajectories are eligible for memory insertion while also providing feedback to guide future proposals. Experiments on AppWorld, BFCL v3, and MCP-Universe show that Preping substantially improves over a no-memory baseline and achieves performance competitive with strong playbook-based methods built from offline or online experience, with deployment cost $2.99\times$ lower on AppWorld and $2.23\times$ lower on BFCL v3 than online memory construction. Further analyses reveal that the main benefit does not come from synthetic volume alone, but from proposer-side control over feasibility, redundancy, and coverage, combined with selective memory updates.

</details>


### [176] [Conditional Attribute Estimation with Autoregressive Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14004)
*Erica Stutz,Giacomo Marino,Daniella Meeker,Qiao Liu,Andrew J. Loza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出条件属性变换器（CAT），通过联合估计下一个词元概率和每个候选词元对应的属性值，在单次前向传播中实现逐词元信用分配、反事实分析和可控生成，在稀疏奖励任务上达到SOTA，并显著加速属性概率估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于next-token prediction训练的自回归模型在下游应用中需要估计或控制序列级属性，但next-token prediction会导致局部模式过拟合和全局结构欠拟合，推理时需要大量下游修改或昂贵采样才能引导或预测生成样本的全局属性。

Method: 提出Conditional Attribute Transformers，在单次前向传播中联合估计下一个词元概率以及每个候选词元条件下的属性值，无需修改输入序列即可实现三种能力：(1)逐词元信用分配；(2)基于替代词元选择的条件属性差异量化（反事实分析）；(3)结合词元概率和属性似然进行可控解码。

Result: 在稀疏奖励任务上达到SOTA；在足够模型规模下提升了next-token prediction性能；属性概率估计速度比采样方法快数个数量级；能够在多种语言任务上引导自回归序列模型的解码过程。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了next-token prediction训练范式下序列级属性估计与控制困难的问题，在效率、性能和可控性方面均取得显著优势。

Abstract: Generative models are often trained with a next-token prediction objective, yet many downstream applications require the ability to estimate or control sequence-level properties. Next-token prediction can lead to overfitting of local patterns during training, underfitting of global structure, and requires significant downstream modifications or expensive sampling to guide or predict the global attributes of generated samples at inference time. Here, we introduce Conditional Attribute Transformers, a novel method for jointly estimating the next-token probability and the value of an attribute conditional on each potential next token selection. This framework enables three critical capabilities within a single forward pass, without modification of the input sequence: (1) per-token credit assignment across an entire sequence, by identifying how each token in a sequence is associated with an attribute's value; (2) counterfactual analysis, by quantifying attribute differences conditional on alternative next token choices; (3) steerable generation, by decoding sequences based on a combination of next-token and attribute likelihoods. Our approach achieves state of the art performance on sparse reward tasks, improves next-token prediction at sufficient model sizes, estimates attribute probabilities orders of magnitude faster than sampling, and can guide decoding of autoregressive sequence models on a range of language tasks.

</details>


### [177] [Sheaf-Theoretic Transport and Obstruction for Detecting Scientific Theory Shift in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14033)
*David N. Olivieri,Roque J. Hernández*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于有限层理论的框架，用于检测AI科学智能体中的理论转移候选信号。通过源图表、重叠图表、目标图表和验证图表的局部到全局结构，测量表征运输的阻塞程度（残差拟合、重叠不兼容、约束违反、极限关系失败和表征成本），并在过渡卡片基准测试上验证了该方法：预期的变形或扩展通常是阻塞程度最低的候选方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体进行科学理论转移时，单纯拟合数据是不够的，必须检测现有表征框架在新条件下是否仍然可运输，或者其语言是否已从局部到全局受阻而需要扩展。现有方法缺乏对此类诊断子问题的形式化处理。

Method: 构建有限层理论框架：将语境组织为局部到全局结构，包含源图表、重叠图表、目标图表和验证图表；通过拟合、限制和胶合测试评估相干性；定义五种阻塞测量指标（残差拟合、重叠不兼容、约束违反、极限关系失败、表征成本）；引入星座核作为次要的表征相似性探针。在可控过渡卡片基准上进行评估。

Result: 主要结果是直接阻塞排名：预期的变形或扩展通常是阻塞程度最低的候选方案，且过渡类型在基准测试中能够被区分。

Conclusion: 本文并非旨在重构历史范式迁移或解决开放式自主理论发明，而是为AI智能体隔离出一个有限的诊断子问题：检测表征运输何时失败以及扩展何时成为连贯的下一步行动。

Abstract: Scientific theory shift in AI agents requires more than fitting equations to data. An artificial scientific agent must detect whether an existing representational framework remains transportable into a new regime, or whether its language has become locally-to-globally obstructed and must be extended. This paper develops a finite sheaf-theoretic framework for detecting theory-shift candidates through transport and obstruction. Contexts are organized as a local-to-global structure in which source, overlap, target, and validation charts are fitted, restricted, and tested for gluing. Obstruction measures failure of coherence through residual fit, overlap incompatibility, constraint violation, limiting-relation failure, and representational cost. We evaluate the framework on a controlled transition-card benchmark designed to separate deformation within a source language from extension of that language. The main result is direct obstruction ranking: the intended deformation or extension is usually the lowest-obstruction candidate, and transition type is separated in the benchmark. A constellation kernel over the same signatures is included only as a secondary representational-similarity probe. The aim is not to reconstruct historical paradigm shifts or solve open-ended autonomous theory invention, but to isolate a finite diagnostic subproblem for AI agents: detecting when representational transport fails and extension becomes the coherent next move.

</details>


### [178] [Enhanced and Efficient Reasoning in Large Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14036)
*Leslie G. Valiant*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种高效的、基于一元关系整合编码（Unary Relational Integracode）的预处理方法，通过学习数据中显式的关系表示，使大规模语言模型能够以可计算负担的方式进行有原则的推理，支持分类器内部及多次调用间的可靠逻辑链，并在多项式时间内可学习核心关系规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型虽能生成流畅文本，但缺乏有原则的依据来证明其生成内容的可信度。普遍认为通过增加有原则的推理来解决此问题在计算上不可行，本文旨在探索一种计算高效的推理方法。

Method: 两阶段方法：第一阶段进行预处理，将数据重新编码为更明确表达对象间关系的一元关系整合编码（Unary Relational Integracode）；第二阶段采用标准但可能简化的机器学习过程，同时学习预测这些关系。该方法可视为实现一种世界模型，并适用于自然语言以外的视觉和动作等场景。

Result: 该编码方式具有令人意外的有利特性：在保持简洁的同时，使得学习训练数据中描述的世界所包含的核心关系规则子集在多项式时间内可学习（多项式复杂度取决于规则的复杂度）。这为单次分类器调用内及多次调用间的可靠推理提供了支持。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法在保持大部分现有软硬件基础的前提下，通过显式编码关系信息，使得大规模语言模型能够以可计算负担的方式进行有原则的推理，克服了传统方法中计算不可行的难题。

Abstract: In current Large Language Models we can trust the production of smoothly flowing prose on the basis of the principles of machine learning. However, there is no comparably principled basis to justify trust in the content of the text produced. It appears to be conventional wisdom that addressing this issue by adding more principled reasoning is not computationally affordable.
  Here we propose a principled method of reasoning that is efficient enough to be practical for large language models. Further, the method allows the retention of much of the currently used software and hardware base. Our method for improving the functioning of large language models consists of a first stage of preprocessing that recodes the data to a Unary Relational Integracode that is more explicit about the relationships among the objects described in the text, followed as a second stage by a standard but possibly streamlined machine learning process that then also learns to predict these relationships.
  The method may be viewed as realizing a world model and applying beyond natural language, to vision and actions, for example, where the multiple properties of an object referred to in an input are brought together explicitly, rather than remaining distributed in the various references to it in the input. We articulate its advantages in terms of Robust Logic, a system for performing principled chaining on learned, and hence uncertain, information. We show that this recoding has the surprising and fortuitous property that, while succinct, it makes the task of learning a core subset of relational rules that hold in the world described in the training data polynomial time learnable in a defined sense, the polynomial depending on the complexity of the rule. This gives support for sound reasoning within each single call of the learned classifier as well as between multiple calls.

</details>


### [179] [Model-Adaptive Tool Necessity Reveals the Knowing-Doing Gap in LLM Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14038)
*Yize Cheng,Chenrui Fan,Mahdi JafariRaviz,Keivan Rezaei,Soheil Feiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种**模型自适应的工具必要性定义**，并发现LLM在工具调用中存在显著的"知-行差距"：模型虽能在隐状态中编码工具需求，但将其转化为实际工具调用行为时存在大量失败，且这一失败主要发生在认知到行动的转换阶段，而非认知本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
